# Continuity

Rules of record. Anything below is canon. If a batch needs to change one of these, record the change here in the same commit, with the chapter it changed in.

## World rules

1. Cultivation has seven realms of nine stages: Body-Forging, Condensing, Foundation, Golden Core, Nascent Soul, Severance, Immortal Vessel. The Last Ascension is a thirteenth thing, not a realm.
2. Advancing past stage nine of a realm inside the bound requires a sect **seal** as well as a body. Servants are not given it.
3. Advancing inside the bound is **announced** to everyone in the bound at once. There is no private breakthrough.
4. Announcements are audible, never written. They report and never advise.
5. Announcements **can be edited** before they are broadcast. Three counties' copies of one announcement may differ. Discovered at the Volume 1 midpoint.
6. Death by harvest leaves an **account** that follows a family three generations.
7. Qi is finite. Running dry is the most common cause of death under thirty.
8. Nothing inside the bound repairs an aperture. Damage is permanent and visible.
9. **The world has no named weeks and no named weekdays.** A day is an hour of the day and a day of a month; a month is thirty days, so month-day = `((day − 134) mod 30) + 1`, day 134 being the first of a month. Characters say *four days up and four days down*, *the third of the month*, *the second hour of the afternoon*. **Narration may not name a weekday, in any register and from any mouth.** Written into canon at the Volume 4 close review; all eight standing instances in Volumes 1 and 2 were repaired the same day.

## The layer's rules

1. Yuan can read every cultivator inside the bound and touch nothing. No hands, no body, no qi.
2. He can **designate** one Chosen One at a time, anywhere inside the bound. Designating costs him the memory of his own name.
3. The layer honours a Chosen One's quests. Rewards land. Qi answers her a half-step faster.
4. The layer cannot be lied to about a quest's cost, and neither can Yuan.
5. He cannot grant what he cannot pay for.
6. She can refuse him. A refused designation begins to rot and he cannot stop it.
7. **Settlement** rises every time he uses the channel. It only falls when **Merit** is spent.
8. Merit is spent from **her foundation**. It does not come back. She gets stronger and less solid at once.
9. The tax takes **sequence**, not substance: the technique and the order, not the body's capacity. Practice rebuilds substance and cannot rebuild order.
10. He can act through her hand and body. It costs more than the tax and it is partial.
11. He can read the shape of a technique on a living person and **cannot describe it in words**. He can only find it.
12. He is read by the layer, not reading it. He cannot see who writes it back.
13. The designation holds whether or not she speaks to him and whether or not she wants it (ch 37).
14. Advancements are announced in a fixed four-clause order — where, who, what was let go, what is held — and that order is the order a body must be moved in. Empty first, then take (ch 40, ch 43).
15. Something walks a row in the layer once a day and looks at him. Four or five breaths. Never missed. It asks nothing and takes nothing (ch 43).
16. A cracked aperture is a channel as well as a bill. Qi that leaks sideways into a held pattern does work, and is paid for at the rim on every use (ch 39).

## Registers

- Yuan: every sentence in the **present tense**. No other character is ever in the present tense.
- The layer: square-bracket lines, on their own line, **at most one per chapter**. Never bold, never italics, never a heading.
- Chapter markdown: title heading, optional section headings, plain paragraphs, scarce bold/italic. No blockquotes, no code blocks, no tables, no horizontal rules.

## Prose

- Paragraphs two to four sentences. Break over about 90 words. Never over about 120.
- Chapters land around 1,800–2,700 words unless the scene needs longer. **Floor 1,300.** A batch averaging under 1,300, or whose word counts fall monotonically across the batch, is a failed batch: add scene material inside the existing cards, never pad. Never record a length trade-off.
- **Never put outline language inside a character's thought or narration** — *this chapter, this volume, the next chapter, in this batch, the whole series, the reader*. Say what the character knows. This leaked four times in batch 0008 and twice more in closed Volume 1; all are now gone.
- Long accumulating sentences are the voice, but a revelation gets short plain sentences at the point of impact.
- One simple sentence about one person after any stretch of machinery.
- Never: "his eyes widened", "he couldn't believe it", "suddenly", "little did he know".
- Construction watch: *nobody asked, nobody was thanked, there is no form, there is no column, the record remained, nobody knew.* Effective once or twice each, then dead. Used in batch 0001: furnace column (ch 1,12). Do not reuse without new meaning.

## Content

- No explicit sexual content. Intimacy is earned and goes off page.
- Ning Yan is seventeen in Volume 1 and the book does not romanticise that. Nothing in Volumes 1 and 2 is a romance.
- All sexual characters are adults.
- No intimacy involving Yuan. He has no body and that is not a problem to be solved.
- Real people do not appear as characters.

## Named and reserved

Yuan (was Yuan Sheng), Ning Yan, Ning Xue, Ning Bo, Ning Wu, Qiu Zhulang, Bai Zhu, Shuyin, Du Hong, Mei Jiu, Shen Xiu, Feng Lushi, Wei Ran, Wen the lamp-mender. Sects: Qinghe, Beihe, Canglan, Luoyuan, Shouyi, Wanzhou, Hanhui, Dushou, Tianshu. Places: Nine Steps Terrace, Lowwater, the Ashwood Verge, the Broken Kiln, the Lantern Register tower. Institutions: the Bureau of Records, the Lantern order, the Elder's Hall, the Warden's office, the Order of the Recruited, the Scribe.

## Established facts (through ch 24)

- Ning Yan: Body-Forging 3 (ch 1–2) → 9 briefly ch 3 → **4** ch 3–4 → 5 ch 8 → 6 ch 11 → 7 ch 14 (yard losing/work) → 8 ch 22 (4-mile carry + no sleep, tax untouched). Scar sternum to hip ch 3, permanent. Ashwood Nine-Step lost as technique ch 3, habit survives ch 4/14. → 9 ch 26 (locate-work) → **4** ch 36 (through-hand spend, order taken, Nine-Step habit gone). Second scar left shoulder-hip ch 36. Aperture rim cracked (unknown) → cracked through ch 36 permanent.
- Ch 13: furnace column is account, 3-gen rule, settled in kind/service; billed 6 years in air. Du Hong protects from specific number he does not know.
- Ch 14 (deadline): winter drill in 9 days. First deliberate omission between them (asks what else he took, true+incomplete, both let stand). Log foreshadowed.
- Ch 15: past stage 9 needs sect seal/sponsor; servants cannot be sealed. Map absent not locked. Mei Jiu not bad, own account, seal cost.
- Ch 16: layer is office not sky, somebody speaks/writes it; Bureau is visible part. Ning chooses not to know (first time).
- Ch 17: Feng 400 vols, index in head, hand degrading plot fact. Fourth column no heading; Ning Wu in it.
- Ch 18: Warden's marker post (same Vol 4). Wen cart stops, cannot cross (unable not unwilling). Kiln sealed by layer.
- Ch 19: 40 min truly-alone, only this volume, metallic taste. Yuan absent not blocked, returns count wrong.
- Ch 20 onward exact wording: *assessed, reassigned, unreconciled*. Kiln assesses/manufactures.
- Ch 21 irreversible: decides aloud to empty room to take tablet. Tablet single fired plate size of hand, surface like skin (described once, remembered Vol 16).
- Ch 22: removal logged [Removal from sealed ground is logged.]; first thing out 16 years. 7→8 carry, tax untouched noticed. Settlement rises again. She does not ask pay / he never volunteers (pattern to Vol 5).
- Ch 23: Wen will not touch (professional disgust). Tablet made of people / core-stone fired qi. Wears against skin rest Vol 1, never comfortable.
- Ch 24: Yuan can act through her hand (first time in 19 years he picks anything up), slower costs more. Log started in own hand clerks cannot read quickly. Settlement spikes, loses mother's name (name itself, not grief; never says again, never says forgotten). Qiu waits — unresolved.
- Ch 25: log is stolen laundry ledger back pages, deliberately poor hand, wash-code inside learner's strokes, rubbish-camouflage. Seed Vol 4 + Feng fair-hand Wall discovery.
- Ch 26: Body-Forging 8→9 via work + locate. Yuan reads shape of technique off living person, cannot describe in words, only locates. Used Vol 7/13. Winter drill in 3 days met.
- Ch 27: Shen Xiu to report for son's clerkship/inner place; reports ch 34 three days early, never forgiven. Tired/honest not villain.
- Ch 28: Qiu 61, kind, keeps five names in head not paper, gives all five freely. One not servant — plant, Vol 5 payoff.
- Ch 29: Ning Wu *reassigned, signed above furnace authority, in hand Qiu does not know*. Feng recognises Vol 5. Hunt points upward.
- Ch 30: offer real — furnace crew servant-rank inner, bed indoors, stipend, family account written off if she stops asking. Refused then accepted ch 31 as trap-inside-kindness. Second offer logged Vol 4 echo.
- Ch 31: midpoint reversal — Qiu promotes to furnace crew in Hall, approved 11 min, genuine. Bed, stipend, account deferred not settled.
- Ch 32: furnace procedure live = kiln wall word for word, ordinary horror. Two crew friends made, unnamed, both die Vol 2.
- Ch 33: Du Hong inner by her recommendation, sent to report in 3 days, has not. Pallet paper → Feng plan. Dies Vol 2 because of her — start.
- Ch 34: irreversible consent — barred records room, Shen reports early, only way through her, full through-hand cost stated, she says do it. First consented tax; never again until Vol 16.
- Ch 35: Qiu not ordering barring, comes down on own authority, stands between Hall men and door, loses argument on purpose. Not coward/martyr.
- Ch 36: 9→4, aperture cracked through permanent, second scar left shoulder-hip, permanently weaker rest of book. Settlement over half. Tax is withdrawal taking what is on top. Signature Bureau hand nobody recognises; Feng will recognise. Assembly in 19 days, no seal.
- Layer brackets used: ch 6,7,8,22,36, and one in every chapter 37–46. All other chapters zero.
- Records room visited ch 34–35. Broken Kiln interior done 19–21.

## Established facts, batch 0004 (ch 37–46)

- Ning Yan: stage 4 ch 36 → **5** ch 39 (second day of the eleven) → **6** ch 39 (sixth day) → **7** ch 39 (seventh day, announced bound-wide) → **8** ch 40 (carrying forty-one volumes up the Lowwater road) → **9** ch 43 (terrace step, by deliberate emptying, unaided) → **sealed for Condensing** ch 45.
- Aperture: cracked through, permanent, never heals. **Treated as a channel** ch 39. Deliberate emptying (ch 43) empties from the legs down and leaves a hollow chest that refills by rest. Two fingers of the right hand unreliable; not permanently numb.
- The tax takes order, not substance (ch 38). She named it: *you took the order the thing was in.* Yuan does not understand this about himself until Volume 11.
- Partnership survives ch 36 (ch 37): not forgiven, employed. She keeps him because he is the only person who has lied to her in a way she could check.
- Crack-as-channel is hers and unshared — Du Hong, Mei Jiu, Qiu and the log entry under wash code are the only ones who know. Used in Volume 7 against the Hunter.
- Feng Lushi ch 40: ordered by the Bureau (eight days prior) to omit her from the upward copy and has not omitted her; has copied four entries upward in the third column of the middle register, which nobody reads. Gave her the four-clause order out of eleven volumes. Has not been given the four county sheets. Four copies upward pay off Vol 11; they make him a traitor in Vol 3.
- Lowwater Watch ch 41: Captain **Lu Tao**, 41–46, keeps a date and a shelf mark from the log on the back of his own form. **Old Gao**, water-carrier for Ning Xue, put a hand on a young Watchman's arm in the roundhouse. Captain's household on the register: noted in the log, deliberately not used. Both return Vol 2 and Vol 5.
- Ning Yan reads a log page aloud in the Lowwater market square, first crowd on her side, and uses leverage for the first time. It tastes like Yuan and she is frightened of that. She gives the captain up to Qinghe instead of finishing him.
- Layer is editable before broadcast (ch 42). One hour, four sentences: Lowwater/Feng wrote the plain report; Hanhui added *for discipline*; Shouyi omitted her name; Wanzhou wrote *a servant of Qinghe*. Hanhui sheet carries a county stamp. Three sheets taken from the Lowwater roll bin by a copyist named Han and a woman who would not give her name. **This is the Volume 1 midpoint turn and the clue that opens the Volume 2 document plot; the Bureau hand on the reassignment page pays off in Volume 5.**
- Yuan is read, not reading (ch 43). He cannot see where a sentence is decided. He is more frightened of this than she is. She saw him as a readout for one breath during the stage-nine report — **she has not told him and will not before Volume 11.**
- Ch 44: **Clerk Xie Shuo** rules there is no precedent and then rules anyway, because a non-ruling travels upward. Rules that where the Hall's own objection prevents a seal, the Hall's objection **is** the writ. She refuses Qiu's offered sponsor's writ in front of eleven witnesses; a favour is revocable. He is stripped of the furnace in ch 50; Xie Shuo is ruined by Qiu's faction for the ruling.
- Ch 45: the second offer is worse than the first. Inner rank, bed, stipend, family account struck on the day of signature, Foundation at the Hall's cost, the Clay Tablet surrendered — for a signature stating she has never entered sealed ground and never carried kiln clay. Delivered by a servant-rank clerk who said out loud that he only carried it. She does not destroy it. She logs it word for word with the date, the clerk, and the pin hole. **Returns as evidence in Volume 4.** Seal came from the Hall's own finding, three witnesses, at the furnace floor, fourth hour of the eighth day.
- Ch 46: **nine hundred and eleven witnesses.** Objection by Elders Lu Kang, Deng Chao and Pei An disposed of on its own last line. Petition read aloud on the Assembly floor — her name, in a room, for the second time in the volume; the first was a ledger. She petitions that the layer speak, wins it on the four county sentences, and converts a plea into a procedure. **Trial: Assembly floor, in four days, ninth hour, announced to the bound.** She has not ascended.

## Established facts, batch 0005 (ch 47–58) — VOLUME 1 CLOSED

- Ning Yan: Body-Forging 9 sealed (ch 45) → **Condensing stage 1, ch 52**, on the fifth strike, by opening the aperture crack *outward* instead of taking a shape inward. **The Assembly Hall roof comes off in ch 52 and is never rebuilt** (open floor used Vol 5; still no roof Vol 6).
- **Right arm struck out of function in ch 52**; returns about a third of the time. Fever broken by ch 55. Aperture stabilised at the crack: no longer a hole or a rim, a mouth, for the rest of the book.
- **Mei Jiu's compression (ch 47):** an unwritten inner technique for a narrow gate, given for nothing, never explained in Volume 1 (explained Vol 8). Deliberately **not** written into the log, so that an unwritten compression cannot be found in a Bureau hand. Mei Jiu's motive stated aloud: she would rather the Hall be frightened of what a refusal costs than compete.
- **Ash fall (ch 46 →):** eleven days, does not settle. Days 1–11 are ch 47 (d1), 48 (d2), 49 (d3), 50–54 (d4, trial), 55 (d5–7), 56 (d8), 57–58 (d9, first grey snow). Ends two days after ch 58.
- **Qiu Zhulang stripped ch 50** on a procedural vote: the entry of the eleventh day and eleven minutes is retrospective and therefore void; he refuses to plead it because pleading would void her rank with his. Vacated in four minutes. Pensioned at the third estate, seat retained, right to be heard on furnace matters and no vote. Says one sentence to her on the way out and nothing else all morning: *I have never once been asked what I am allowed to think.* Not repeated word for word again until the final chapter.
- **"The shape of a happy life"** — Qiu's phrase, about a lamp shop in Lowwater, spoken once in ch 50. Reserved by `bible/terminology.md` and `outline/ending.md` for Ning Wu in Vol 16. **Do not use again before then.**
- **Xie Shuo** read the record in ch 53 and entered her brother's word in ch 56. Ruined quietly by Qiu's faction off page after ch 56; gone from the Hall's table. Not a martyr, no speech.
- **Announcement wording, fixed ch 53, to be quoted word for word in ch 1000:** *Assembly Hall of Qinghe. Ning Yan of the Qinghe furnace crew, servant-born. What was held is let go. What was taken is held.*
- **The four seconds (ch 53–54), fixed:** a report is made and then *released*, and the release is the act of whoever is holding the sentence; the release cannot be compelled by anyone in the hall. Every advancement in nine counties for a hundred years went out the instant it was ready, so a delay is the visible edge of a decision. Yuan held it four seconds and let it go. **He never says so.** She derives it from the rule alone in ch 54 and neither of them says the number out loud.
- **Why he let it go (ch 54):** a suppressed breakthrough reads as nothing having happened; nothing having happened is the shape a furnace looks for. A public Condensing 1 is in four offices' books in nine counties and cannot be removed quietly inside the month. **This is the irreversible decision of Volume 1 and the payment for ch 37.** He made it about her, without asking. She understood him completely, did not forgive him, set a rule that he decide nothing about her again without telling her, and stayed.
- **Settlement** passed two-thirds in ch 54. **Ledger** was offered for about ninety seconds and **refused**; he permanently lost the ability to read past her own skin. Running tally: risen five times, fallen twice.
- **The layer has a name for him** (ch 54): **Yuan Sheng**, the name he had before he died. He does not know what he was and does not until Volume 11. She used it out loud in ch 54, ch 57 and ch 58, against his instruction, and said she would be wrong about it again.
- **Ch 55 — the four offices.** Her father's furnace column is reopened in four county books, each adding *Credit. Condensing, stage one. Held*, and each billing the household twice more (entered as two years carried, against her own stage-7). Being famous cost Ning Bo eight more years. One advancement entered in four counties is billed four times.
- **Ch 55 — the Chosen One bounty.** The bound entered the first claim in its history against the Chosen One: eight hundred jin, proof defined as a body, a place and a name. It cannot be withdrawn by the designation. Yuan did not enter it; he made her one. Protection and danger are the same line in the same four books.
- **Ch 55 — Du Hong, and the thing she was holding.** She tells him on the sixth step that she has known since before the trial that the Hall sent him to report her; he filed nothing; and she has never once mentioned it in any room, which is the only reason no office has ever asked him why. She was furious with him about the coat and still is. He refuses to let her make it a favour: she is going to put it on the record, that puts her name in his file, and with an eight hundred jin claim standing that makes him the cheap door and her the expensive one. She says yes.
- **Ch 56 — the second entry.** At the second hour and forty-four minutes she has a second entry taken on the same sponsorship sheet in her own hand: Du Hong advanced to inner rank at the winter session on her recommendation, directed by this Hall to report her, filed nothing, returned nothing, and the fact that she has held that direction since before the trial and has entered nothing until this hour. Lu Kang allows it because a maintaining member may dictate from the hour she signs; Xie Shuo reads it back and enters it. **He knows she knows, as of this chapter** — the leverage ch 53 said was the only thing between him and ruin has been spent on purpose. **Permanent cost:** two files carry her hand instead of one.
- **Ch 56 — the signature.** Sponsorship signed at the second hour and thirty-one minutes of the fifth day, forty-one witnesses, roofless hall. She gave the schedule of the route to the Broken Kiln in her own readable hand and then swore, falsely and on the record, that the plate had not been in her possession since the hour she left sealed ground — **the first thing she has ever done that is not strictly true.** The Elder Hall has the location and never sees the object. She also signed the clause barring her from ever mentioning the voice, knowing a Chosen One cannot be bound by it and that it binds her by hand. **Account of Ning Bo struck whole. Brother's register reads *reconciled*, entered with the hour, the clerk's name and the fact that it was asked for and not offered.** Iron maintaining members' token given and unwanted.
- **Ch 57 — what he told her, and it is not enough.** The designation is a *writ*, not a reward: the layer can be induced by one person and by nobody else. There have been **eleven designations before hers across six hundred years** and there is a seam at each one that he cannot see past. **A designation can be set aside** by a request from outside the bound, without her agreement, and the office attached to the walking has a name he has never once been able to read. He does not explain the designation's purpose; he refuses to finish, on purpose, when she tells him to.
- **Ch 57 — the first limit.** *Not tonight. You have spent enough of me tonight.* It works, for the first time in four months, and he has never been stopped before. She keeps him on three stated terms: employed not forgiven; the work is her brother; nothing decided about her again without telling her first.
- **Ch 58 — the number.** He has been read once a day since the first night, fifty-eight days, and had believed it began when she arrived (ch 43). The count proves it did not: the row has been coming down past him since before he had a Chosen One. **Something outside the bound is reading a System, and the office that does it has a name he cannot read.** Not named in the text; the Winged Office is still available for Vol 2–3.
- **Log additions (ch 55, 56):** the account-opened-again entry; the wording of the *reconciled* entry; four lines in her own hand without code, ending *there is a plate against my chest* and *I gave a building the road to it and kept the road's reason.*
- **Ch 58:** the four county sheets sent down to Lowwater **in copies in her own hand** with Du Hong, to the Lantern Register tower, no name on it. The originals stay in the log. Two men are on the Qinghe gate who were not on it before ch 56 and one of them asks Du Hong what his file says: the second entry has already made him somebody to stop.

## Established facts, batch 0006 (ch 59–72) — Volume 2 opens

- **Condensing stage 1 for all fourteen chapters. No breakthrough, no tribulation, no announcement of advancement, no power gained and no power lost.** Yuan stays at Voice for the whole batch. The only bracketed layer line in the batch is the two deaths at ch 71.
- **Ch 59 — the fourth bridge.** The pass ices between the second and the fourth hour of the tenth day; the road opens for **market days only** (ch 61 is the first, carts before the ninth hour, feet after), then as it is cleared below the cut. **Captain Lu Tao has `Ning Yan. Held.` written on the Lowwater Watch barrier board in his own hand, with no order on it and no clerk's name against it** — he made that decision himself. He says why out loud: a claim of eight hundred jin stands in the layer against the Chosen One and one person inside this wall is that name. Old Gao holds her at the trestle politely and for her own safety and will carry a letter up for free. **She is on the mountain for the whole batch.**
- **Ch 60 — the roster rule, stated once.** The crew roster is written up on the second evening, goes to the quota office on the third, and **any man of the crew can put his mark against a shift; a signed shift is a legal fact and the floor is checked against a mark, not a body.** Cao Lei and Xu An (the two friends planted ch 32 — back stair, oil ration) sign for her from the eleventh day. Stated once here by Cao Lei in the quarters; demonstrated at ch 65, 66, 71, 72. **Not explained again anywhere.** Ch 65 once mis-sourced it to a clause in a book; repaired.
- **Ch 60 — the folded sheet, and it is paid off at ch 61.** A sheet folded in three on the Hall table at the second night hour of the eleventh day, three characters on the outside of the fold in a careful clerk's hand, **her name in the wrong order**, written by an office that had gone and found out what to write. **Ch 61:** she puts it in front of the new Hall clerk. It was on the Hall table at the west end with the queue papers. His is a copyist's hand — *it is not a hand that writes names, it is a hand that writes down what it has been given.* She tells him to write her name and **he writes it in the wrong order, because he has no other version to work from.** An office has written her name down wrong and now a clerk reproduces the error on request.
- **Ch 61 — THE DOCUMENT.** The **Licence to Practise Past Registration**, issued at the **Bureau of Records' county office at Lowwater** under **Clause Four of the Servant Advancement Settlement** (not Clause Nine — ch 41 spent that). Registrar **Sun He**, nineteen years holding the county book, named once on this page and not again in the batch. Clause Four: a servant-born who breaks in the open may draw sect qi, take paid work outside the crew, and be taught past her stage **only under licence countersigned at the county**. **Three legs and no part is the licence: the sponsors' seal, quota clearance, the county countersignature.** The licence is **per licence, not one fixed number** — the ceiling is set per licence and is unknown to the Hall clerk. **Feng Lushi's name is on the county's standing sheet as a witness of standing** — she is told this is procedure and says *he is a procedure*, and does not go further in Volume 2.
- **Ch 61 — quota clearance is counted in hours.** Every hour worked in the crew counts against the household and toward the year. The hours go into **Shen Xiu's winter book**, kept by his office, **not the Hall**. The clerk who writes it writes slowly on purpose, because a fast number makes the book unbalanced at month's end. She signs with her left hand and enters herself as labour toward her own clearance.
- **Ch 62 — the plate clause, stated once.** The sponsorship requires **a single fired plate the size of a hand, returned to the maintaining members within the week of signature**. The clause **fell due at the noon hour of the twelfth day** (signed day 5, seven days). It **cannot be late**, because her own sworn entry at the second hour and thirty-one minutes of the fifth day says she cannot produce it and does not undertake to. **The ch 56 falsehood has become a trap with her hand on one side of it and theirs on the other.** What the sponsors want is the furnace schedule worked on their account and her mouth shut about the voice, **in that order**. Sealing the application **enters her name in the county queue — a public fact.**
- **Ch 63 — Feng.** Du Hong delivered the four county copies on the second day of the market. **Feng read all four, put them behind the fifth item in the copying room, and entered none of them upward.** He said *do not thank me for it.* His unindexed column stays at **five**. He is not thanked.
- **Ch 63 — the proof-hunt, advanced once.** A man has been to **three county desks on the Lowwater square (not the tower)** asking which clerk keeps the copies of the Qinghe advancement, and has been paying. Two of three proofs asked for aloud. **The bounty has moved on from wordings to records — the ears are not re-revealed, the direction they turned is.**
- **Ch 63 / ch 68 — the claim is an entered act. GIVEN ONCE, AT CH 68.** Ch 63 is where Yuan **fails to give it**: pressed on the terrace on the fourteenth day he says *both* — he has not got it, and what he has got is worse than nothing — and says it does not come out of a ledger or a list but out of **a man asked in daylight who has had to answer in front of people who will remember it**, and that there is exactly one place on that mountain where a man will do that. **He refuses to say it from above a crowd: *I have been wrong in a room before and I have never once been wrong from above a crowd. Ask me when there is one.*** Ch 68 is that asking, and the whole of the arithmetic is given there, once: a claim in the bound is entered by the layer, against something, and **a man who works an entered act is working inside it.** **Neither of them says the word *listed* aloud.** Do not give it a third time.
- **Ch 64 — the tithe table.** Three planks four feet from the furnace mouth, with a flat-cut brush and a book the size of a door. The clerk is about twenty-three with a broken finger set an inch too long and a worse cough than hers. **Above the sheet, six unsigned lines in a fair small hand: an hour worked is an hour asked, and the hours are entered against the household and count toward the year.** Delivered on the fourth day past with a new book. **Twelve unsigned sheets in a frame at the back of the quota office; she sees them at ch 69 and enters them in the log in wash code.** **Held for Volume 7, not used again in this volume.**
- **Ch 64 / 67 / 72 — the hours she has entered with her own hand.** Her credited shift is the ninth hour to the second night hour and **the floor's board carries it at four and a half hours.** Day 15: she signs the tithe sheet for **four and a half hours** (ch 64). Day 16, ninth hour: the Hall clerk certifies the schedule and reads her hours for the eleventh to the seventeenth as **four and one-half**, the rest being the two men who sign for her (ch 65). Day 17: the tithe clerk reads back **four and a half hours**, and Cao Lei's and Xu An's sheets carry the same figure with the note *standing against a member's shift, certified*. **Her running total entered personally is nine hours.** Ch 72 puts the refused licence slip under the cover of the tithe book so that a quota clerk opening that book in about nine weeks counts nine hours against a member and finds county paper under his wrist.
- **Ch 65 — the maintaining member's consent regulation, stated once and never explained again.** A maintaining member's effects may not be examined without her consent; **consent may be withheld without reason, the reason need not be given, and it may not be challenged by the instructing party.** She has used the maintaining-member standing since ch 56; this is the first time she has used it **to stop something happening to her.**
- **Ch 66 — the irreversible decision of the batch.** She refuses the examination aloud in the quarters doorway **at ten minutes past the tenth hour** before eleven witnesses, and enters the hour twice — the sheet reads *entered at the tenth hour of the morning of the sixteenth day*, and the burial register at ch 72 records the eighth hour and the tenth. The seal holds. **The inspectors take the crew roster** — the board at the foot of the stair — because a roster is sect property, an instrument silent on a roster takes it, and she has just proved she is not sect property. **They read it aloud against the certified furnace schedule in front of the foreman; in about eleven seconds the foreman doubles Cao Lei and Xu An onto the settling work from the next day to the close of the thaw.** He says he is **short of two**, and the two of them are standing on his stair, which is the whole of his reasoning. Mechanism has no villain and no speech. **She had four moves available and used none of them**, including *take me instead*, because of the plate against her ribs. **She chose two lives that were not hers over one life that was.** The refusal is **entered in the minute book with the hour and the eleven witnesses and is retrievable in Volume 4.** **The plate clause's purpose is revealed by an inspector who tells her, kindly, that the maintaining members already hold the plate described in their own hand — so the clause was written for the oath, not for the plate.**
- **Ch 67 — Hou Sanliang.** The eight hundred jin has a **name**. Read upside down over the clerk's tray: a debt paper naming **Hou Sanliang, of Lowwater**, creditor on **three of the nine struck households**, striking dates **eleven months and eleven days apart** — the same spacing, to the day, as the sponsors' eleven-year schedule — with a fourth line citing **the ledger of the maintaining members of Qinghe, per their schedule of the eleven years.** **The sponsors' quiet book and the broker's paper are the same book.** The ch 55 inference is spent and is not argued again; the name and the document are the new fact. **He is a creditor in this chapter, not a hunter.**
- **Ch 68 — Ledger's price, stated ONCE and not restated at ch 80.** To reach past her skin he would take something back; the last time it went up hard and cost **about four and a half weeks of ordinary use**; **he has about four months left**; and **the spike is paid out of her foundation, does not come back, and comes out of the only thing he has ever had a way of getting at.** He has spent it twice and never told her. **She says *read him* — that is a request, not an order. She does not order it in this chapter and does not offer to pay. The order comes at ch 80 and nowhere earlier.**
- **Ch 69 — the figure.** **Hours asked for a household of her size: 940.** Hours worked and counted to the eighteenth day: **211. Standing: 729.** **940 is a furnace crew's year to the last ten hours**, which is why the eight hundred jin is priced where it is. **Clearance is two numbers and the second one is not hers:** (1) *her line* — the household's worked hours level with its asked hours at the close of the year; (2) *the winter book* — closes square when **every estate standing on it** is level or accounted for, which is his job and has nothing whatever to do with her arm. **The book is currently out by 611 hours and not one of them is on the ninth estate** — they are on five estates she is not told about, and they are the reason he has had no free hand on the closing since the sixth day. **Her 729 is therefore the whole of her obstacle and no part of it is anybody else's arithmetic.** Shen Xiu: clearance is not his to give; a sponsor with a sheet and a seal moves it nothing; **he will not weight the asked figure for her arm and neither can she**; he will not hurry it and has told two Elders what happens if he does and refuses to tell her. **Twenty-three days of thaw will not do 729 hours; the certificate lands in the spring behind the thaw.** **The books say spring** — middle of spring for the certificate, first week of summer for the county to receive it. His early report (ch 27, ch 34) is never spoken aloud. **She pleads nothing.**
- **Ch 69 — the frame, counted correctly.** **Twelve sheets in a frame on the back wall.** One is the hours sheet. **Two are in the roundhouse hand — Captain Lu Tao's hand, from the `Ning Yan. Held.` line on his own barrier board at ch 59 — and the other nine are in four different hands.** All twelve unsigned. Two carry eleven-year spacing on households she has never heard of. Entered in the log in wash code, including the roundhouse hand.
- **Ch 70 — the refusal, fixed here for the rest of the volume.** *Application refused. Quota clearance not produced. The sponsors' seal is of no effect without it and is not opened. A further application may be made upon production of the said clearance. The application is returned entire.* Entered on the queue slip in a clerk's hand at the Hall landing, **with the three sponsors' seals visible on the paper above it to nine people in a queue.** She asked for the county to be named and was told she is the first in two years to ask. **She files once and only once.** She keeps the slip and does not tear it.
- **Ch 71 — the two deaths.** A slag beam over the cold end, nine feet, on four shoring posts, with a hairline in the near post that **she saw and did not think about at ch 70.** A foreman short-handed signs speed over the shoring at the seventh hour; four men are in a hole that takes six. The beam comes down on the doubled shift; both die before the healer reaches the floor forty minutes later. **Mechanism ordinary, agency hers.** She lifts the beam two inches off Xu An alone and holds it eleven seconds and then has to leave it because the channel is coming over and there are **eleven** men on the floor below at that moment; she holds the channel with the crack open until the fourth night hour and **fourteen** people are counted off a stair (eleven crew, two down, one lamp boy). **Yuan is useless and says so in the first second.** She washes their faces before she can speak. **After the deaths the crew is nine men** — every count in ch 71 from the ninth hour of the morning onward and all of ch 72 reads **nine, not eleven**. **The third casualty is carried, not dropped:** a **girl of twenty-four on the cold end** goes off the lip on the same movement with her leg broken, and Ning Yan sits with the leg above the break until the healer comes; at ch 72 she is down at the wash house with the leg in a splint made of a lath and a piece of cord, off the settling floor for the season, and the difference comes out of a household that is not hers. Deaths by furnace accident under a **falsified roster**. **No bounty involvement, no inspectors' involvement, no further invocation of the inspectors' paper after this chapter.**
- **Ch 72 — the burial entry.** Causes named aloud at the burial, in the tally voice, both of them, in front of **nine** men, and **she will not let Yuan put the beam on a different shelf from the refusal.** The burial clerk of about seventy enters nine lines including hers **unasked** and says he has never once written *fell* in that column. **She writes the burial register entry in her own left hand — the first document she has ever written that names her as the cause of anything — ending *and that the undersigned caused both of these things and entered them herself and asks that this entry be read whenever the roster is read*.** It records the refusals as **the eighth hour and the tenth hour of the sixteenth day**. Xu An's mother tells her she will not be at her door and is not saying it as a kindness. She puts the refused licence slip **under the cover of the tithe book with the three seals showing**, and has the clerk write *member on schedule* against her hours. She works the cold end again at the ninth hour. **Cao Lei's wage is six and eight a week, thirteen weeks undrawn**, and an unclaimed quarter of a furnace-crew year is already going down his dead father's unsettled column. **Grief enacted as labour.**
- **The crew is eleven men and her — twelve sleepers — until ch 71, and nine men after it. The crew quarters sleep ten, not twelve.**
- **The register still reads *reconciled*.** The Clay Plate is still against her chest. The iron maintaining-members' token is still in her coat and still unwanted. The log holds the four county originals, the Clause Four copy, the refusal wording, the burial entry and the frame of twelve unsigned sheets.

## Established facts, batch 0007 (ch 73–88) — VOLUME MIDPOINT

- **One stage only: Condensing 1 → 2 at ch 78, announced.** Built from five and a half weeks of one-handed tithe, left-hand form rebuilt from Nine-Step habit (unwritten, unshared), crack-channel practice in the cold. **Yuan's release, not hers** — she is furious, does not stop him, does not thank him. Cost: two weeks fever (ch 79, twelve of fourteen days worked) and a month of short breath that never fully leaves (two counts, stairs cost one stop). The bracketed line is this chapter's only one: Assembly Hall of Qinghe / Ning Yan of the Qinghe furnace crew, servant-born / what is carried past the first is let go / Condensing, stage two, at Qinghe is held. Four-clause order; not the ch 53 wording. No other bracketed line anywhere in the batch. No tribulation.
- **Ledger at ch 80, less than a day, on her order.** She says *read him* as an order (request at ch 68 was not an order). He reaches past her skin through her eyes, reads Hou Sanliang's man: employed by a broker with a counting room, buying records at county desks (Qinghe advancement copies, carriers' papers, **which household in this town she is entered against**), polite, paid by the week. **One instruction carries no coin: a body is not to be asked for, a body is to be waited on.** Pays the spike to **about six and a half weeks of channel**, refuses to hold it again the same day and permanently. **Price paid, not restated.** The order is hers and the only one; Yuan orders himself nothing. The gate clerk tells her afterward that the asking went into a file, and a file is read by whoever the county trusts.
- **Tithe closed as arithmetic, not montage.** Two blocks a week, entered slow by the broken-finger clerk four feet from the furnace mouth. Household: 211/729 (ch 69) → 229/711 (73) → 247/693 (74) → 265/675 (75) → 283/657 (76) → 301/639 (77) → 319/621 (78) → 355/585 (79) → 412/528 (81) → 468/472 (82) → 520/420 (83) → 610/330 (84) → 700/240 (85) → 940/940 (86). Her own signed line, continuous from the day the book opened: 9 → 13.5 → 18 → 22.5 → 27 → 31.5 → 36 → 45 → 58 → 72 → 85 → 102 → 128 → complete. Refused slip stays under the tithe-book cover throughout; *member on schedule* against her hours. **The 412/528 and line 58 figures are stated once, at ch 81 — ch 80 ends on the order and on the clerk, and logs nothing.**
- **Descent: arranged ch 76, used ch 82.** Road open for market traffic on cleared days (carts ninth hour, feet tenth). Barrier at the fourth bridge, board with captain's line. Passage via Old Gao's water cart, entered on the cart sheet, watched not guarded (a guard is a place). **Ch 76 negotiates and does not board** — three cleared days pass with the cart going down without her while the left-hand form is built; **ch 58 is the first arrival.** **Ch 73–81 are on the mountain except that arrival.** Market square, three county desks (advancement copies, carriers' papers, household lines), counting lane. **County office on the square (Sun He, county book, stamp) and copying floor in Feng's tower (copies upward, column five) are different rooms, never merged.** No Qinghe county office. Tower loft with shutter that does not shut; carrying loads under the schedule.
- **Proof-buying answered:** a man in a good coat, about fifty, grey at the temple, employed by a broker with a counting room (paper stacked by household, counting lane). Buys records not wordings, pays for writing as well as answers, daylight, before witnesses. Two proofs worked; place unbought; body unspoken. **Ch 83 — the third proof is not the body. It is the absence.** He pays a county clerk twice for a page saying that a man from the Qinghe furnace crew came down this road and that **no household in this town has her entered against it** — no keeper, no line, no name on a door — because a page that says nobody has her is worth more to him than a page that says where she is. **Live threat, carries into Volume 3.** The county is the only room that can put a line under a person and stand a county hand behind it. **Hou Sanliang and she not in a room in this batch.** Hou known only as creditor on paper (ch 67).
- **Clearance (ch 86):** Shen Xiu signs looking past her, no cruelty, tired, honest. Costs every hour worked and the days not worked; household 940 asked / 940 worked; winter book square including five estates not hers now level or accounted for. No plea, no thanks, no forgiveness. Early report never spoken.
- **Surety (ch 87–88):** Du Hong stands the living surety, told terms (name in county book for every county hour; death voids; debt transfers to her), gives his name aloud, entered. Alive.
- **Midpoint reversal (ch 88):** Certain of licence; Sun He refuses on the bounty standing — a licenced practitioner cannot stand under an unanswered claim. Reads pending filing: hours, stages, defect on the county sheet from the day the book opened, ceiling at Condensing 5. Refusal rhymes ch 70 without quoting it whole. **Irreversible hers:** she enters *aperture cracked through; stabilised at the crack; permanent* in her own hand and signs the ceiling — capped at Condensing 5, barred from Foundation without relicensing — while refused, same hour. Works out the sponsors' Foundation writ dies on the same page (Foundation on it is unregistered practice under a schedule it does not appear on); three sponsors knew and said nothing. Refused application worth nothing; pending filing worth everything except the Foundation. Claim standing and unanswered. On the ground at Lowwater with tower, square, man hunting proofs.
- **Carried threads:** crew nine men, quarters sleep ten; girl of twenty-four alive at wash house, lath-and-cord splint, off floor for season, charged to eighth estate, holds the bunk secret, copies carriers' lists; Cao Lei's quarter-wage kept off his father's column by her hand; foreman signs six and posts six with name on the line, timber sound; name in wrong order unresolved; twelve-sheet frame logged, untouched; eleven designations and outside-bound office untouched (held for ch 96/110); no audit of Feng, no Lung; no second accident/death/frostbite/starvation/rescue; no forgiveness; no hopeful chapter; no harem; Wei Ran unnamed.
- **Titles 73–88:** The Tithe Ledger, What the Body Owes, The Wash House Splint, The Escort She Does Not Have, The Left-Hand Habit, What the Bound Is Told, Work Through Fever, Read Him Aloud, The Counting Room's Employee, The Barrier Escort, Weight of the Market, Nights in the Tower Loft, The Office on the Square, Clearance Without a Look, The Name for Attendance, The Pending Filing. All unique against 1–72. Ch 79 renamed from Fever Work to Work Through Fever for the three-word floor.
- Chapter length: ~1,300 words against the 1,800–2,700 guide. **The "known trade-off" note is retired — see the chapter-length decision in `state/current.md`.** Floor is 1,300; a batch averaging under it, or declining monotonically across its chapters, is a failed batch.

## Established facts, batch 0008 (ch 89–103) — PURSUIT AND THE LOSS

- **One stage only: Condensing 2 → 3 at ch 92, released at her order, announced.** Four weeks of Lowwater carrying work (stone, oil, paper, water) under the schedule, tower loft, built out of load as stage 8 was. Arm settles at a third and stays there. **Cost: announcement is first thing in four months to tell a market where she sleeps.** Bracket this chapter only: *Assembly Hall of Qinghe. Ning Yan of the Qinghe furnace crew, servant-born. What is carried past the second is let go. Condensing, stage three, at Lowwater is held.* Four-clause order; not ch 53 or ch 78 wording. No other bracket except ch 101. No tribulation.
- **The lie (ch 93–94) as exaggeration, not reversal.** He hardens her own ch 55 conclusion (claim cannot take her quietly) into a floor; evidence the eleven — six hundred years, eleven bodies the bound had to account for, designation reason there is a record of any of them at all. Writ not reward stands (ch 57 untouched). Sentence kept: protection and danger are the same line and it is the safe side. She listens, sets no new rule, does not agree aloud. Do not conclude design.
- ***kiln-born* (ch 96) on paper only.** Tower volumes carry a status column: eleven entries across six hundred years, each with a status word, each in a copying hand nobody living uses. Nearest before hers says *kiln-born*. Lowwater county sheet of her advancement carries same word in a second position no other county sheet has; of four hand-copies in tower, one carries it. First new fact about eleven. She speaks the word once to Yuan; he does not recognise it and says so. Do not answer what eleven became. Feng does not resolve hand; column stays five; set-aside/unreadable office untouched (held ch 110).
- **Working-out (ch 100, tower loft, alone).** She works out designation prices her, not shelters her — eleven spaced irregularly because spent at different rates, not found at different times; every honoured quest answers out of same foundation tax draws from, so being Chosen adds occasions for an existing cost. She tells him she worked it out. No new rule; three stand. **She does not forgive him — non-forgiveness runs ch 100 through Volume 3 into Volume 4 (the year).** She does not conclude tax was designed (Volume 11).
- **Attempt, killing, Merit (ch 100–101).** Hou's men stop Du Hong on **Lowwater road at the foot of the Lantern Register tower** — not the pass, one location. He carries her hand in his file and her copies in his coat. He gives his own name instead of hers, burns one sheet, refuses the place. Beaten for the place, left in thaw water at tower foot. She kills the second man with the Nine-Step — habit not technique, one working arm — very nearly killed. **She does not open aperture as channel (held Volume 7).** Entered-act listing exists before kill (ch 68, ch 80); Hou's man on layer list the moment he takes claim up. Merit settles on listed kill — volume's only source, no trial. Yuan tells her **before** he spends it what it costs, what it buys, out of her foundation. She says yes. Third term paid in full, four months late, first time he asks instead of acting. Tax takes sequence not substance: left-hand sequence rebuilt at ch 78 is gone and cannot be relearned. Nothing lost comes back. Settlement back to roughly two-thirds (~11 weeks). Without kill he is dry inside ch 101 (about five days margin at ch 100). Bracket this chapter only: *[The road at Lowwater. One of the entered is ended by her hand.]*
- **Death and burial (ch 102–103).** He dies before night out with Feng holding the lamp. Proof fails on the body; bounty stands unclaimed into Volume 3. She lists four causes at burial, all hers: her hand in his file; her copies in his coat down the road; letting him stand surety; killing one of them when she came down the road. **A fifth, entered at the same time (review repair): that the second man would not have stood where she could reach him had he not been reading his file aloud over a boy in the water.** Feng holds the lamp. Bounty men know cheap door is gone. Surety void, debt hers. **The county now holds the burial line and the debt transfer against her name.**
- **The Watch, night 121 (ch 102).** Arrives at the ninth hour, **not at the killing.** Writes a body, a road, a ditch; that a man was shouting about a refused place and the shouting was finished when it arrived; and that one man was carried up the tower stair alive at the fourth hour with a lamp. **It does not write that the corpse was answering to an entered act in the layer, because no office in Lowwater has a form with a column for it — that is the mechanism by which the eight hundred jin still stands unanswered at dawn.** Her one statement, *entered*, goes in as a statement by the injured party and no more. Do not reopen this; do not let a clerk invent the form.
- **The county receives the paper (ch 103, repair).** At the second hour she lodges the burial line. A clerk asks whether she is a member of the household; she answers *I am the undersigned*, then *It is what I entered*; he writes it into the corner and **stamps it with the same stamp as the pending filing**. He confirms the surety is entered in this county under Du Hong's own name at his own voice; she asks him to write the transfer as **Stands to me** and he writes it in the county book where a county keeps a line. **Two documents with her hand on both, neither about her advancement.**
- **The intake line moved to the ninth hour (ch 95).** The younger man learns **her pace**, not her name — *there is a woman sets the loads down slow.* She has the line moved, **entered by Feng as a tower change and not a request, because a request carries a reason and a change carries a sheet.** Forty hands now share that hour, which is the only reason a slow woman is not a description. Do not put her back on the fifth hour; do not have the change audited.
- **Also on the page after repair:** **the girl of twenty-four, three times** (ch 91 the four-hour correction in person, and the gate man left standing in the water; ch 94, asked directly whether it is safe and answering that she is the safest thing in the town and has been since a winter she does not describe; ch 103, an unasked-for correction slate at a burial); **Feng and the clerks' cover story** — a fall on the stair, carried out to the wash house before the cold got into him — written down by every one of them because it is easier than the other thing; **the counting-room woman** at the third desk, four households read and priced with no hand on her arm (ch 98), which is what moves the pursuit in her own head from bodies to households; **the third desk's sheet goes up at the quarter in the same crate as the rest** (ch 99); **Sun He**, who says the longest sentence he has said to her and it is a clerk telling a holder how to keep a record clean (ch 94).
- **Carried threads:** crew nine, quarters ten; girl of twenty-four alive, splinted, holds bunk secret; tithe line continuous; refused slip under cover; wrong-order name unresolved; twelve-sheet frame logged untouched; pending defect line and ceiling unchanged; writ dead; three men silent; Feng alive, unaudited, no Lung; no second accident/death/frostbite/starvation/rescue; no forgiveness; no hopeful chapter; no harem; Wei Ran unnamed; Hou and she never in a room.
- **Titles 89–103:** The Filing Copy, The Man Who Pays for Writing, Four Weeks of Stone, Where She Sleeps, The Safe Side, Eleven Bodies, What Walks Behind Coin, Kiln-Born, A Word With Nobody, The Cheap Door, Five Days of Margin, What Being Chosen Costs, The Listed Kill, Feng Holding the Lamp, Four Causes. All unique against 1–88.
- **Repaired against `logs/volume-02-batch-0008.review.log`:** 12,176 → **22,269 words**, ~1,483/chapter, floor 1,300. The byte-identical mat/shutter sentence (89/99) removed and the 13-of-15 mat closer broken up. `one word with nobody to hold it` cut to one use (97, the chapter it names). `I touch nothing` cut to one use (90). `I am with you / I am here` cut to four (93 ×2, 97, 99, 101). All authorial scaffolding out of the batch and out of `volume-01/chapter-0019.md` and `chapter-0024.md`. **No title, no chapter order, no card beat, no bracket and no ending changed.**

## Established facts, batch 0009 (ch 104–118) — GRIEF AND THE GRANT. VOLUME 2 CLOSED AND AUDITED.

**Volume 2 close audit — `outline/volume-02.md` walked line by line against the manuscript. PASS
on every named item except the fifteen faults repaired below. Full table and repair note in
`state/current.md` under `## Volume 2 close audit`. No title, chapter order, card beat, bracket or
ending moved. No canon change resulted: every repair moves a figure back onto an existing canonical
fact.**

### Repaired at the Volume 2 close (word-level; post-repair 28,234 words over 15 chapters)

- **The quarters' head count — five places, ch 113, 114, 117, 118.** The batch had drifted to *ten
  men* in a room that canon fixes as **nine men and her = ten sleepers** (ch 73: *Nine men. Ten
  sleepers.*). Ch 118's version explicitly enumerated eleven (*Nine on the crew and one on the
  settling floor and her*) and ch 117 already said *nine men* three other times. **Fixed to *ten
  sleepers* / *nine men breathing* throughout. This is the outline's final image (*ten sleepers
  instead of twelve*) and it now reads true everywhere it appears.**
- **The claim's position on the licence — ch 115, 116.** Called *the fourth condition from the
  bottom* and *four lines from the bottom* three times. **It is the sixth condition and the last
  thing on the paper.** Fixed.
- **The four-century slip — ch 111.** *Four hundred years. Eleven entries.* against six hundred years,
  fixed at ch 57 and restated at 93, 96, 97, 110, 111, 117. **Ch 96's *the last entry was four
  hundred years back* is a different fact (the gap since the previous designation) and stands;
  ch 110's *a thing four hundred years old* refers to the same gap and stands.**
- **Head count of the word *kiln-born* — ch 111.** The plain-text log line made it three heads.
  **It is two** (hers and Yuan's, fixed at ch 110 and ch 117); Feng holds a struck shape and no word.
- **A striking that had not happened yet — ch 113 and ch 115.** Both said the county entered the
  striking *at the second hour this morning*, which is days earlier and four miles away, and in ch 115
  it is the stated reason the fourth stage can never be entered. Rewritten without the false morning.
- **Date clash — ch 111 vs ch 112.** Ch 111 was dated the fourteenth day of the thaw, the same day
  ch 112 puts on the mountain at the other end of a four-mile road. Ch 111's date removed.
- **Weather — ch 114.** *in the snow of a market day*, in a chapter confirmed four times over as
  rain. Fixed.
- **Elapsed time to the tower-foot road — ch 114, 116.** *a month ago* ×3, against a ch 106 → ch 115
  span fixed at six weeks by ch 115's *eleven weeks* and ch 116's *six weeks ago*. Now *two months
  ago*. Ch 106's own *a month ago* stands (three weeks).
- **Elapsed time to the ch 53 announcement — ch 115.** *four months ago* and *in the middle of the
  spring*, both wrong by ~24 weeks and by season; the announcement is in the ash fall, in the winter.
  Now *she had heard it in the winter* and *in a market in the middle of the ash fall*.
- **Residence — ch 116.** *eleven months* in the tower; it is about three.
- **Distance — ch 118.** *eleven hundred yards away down the mountain* against a four-mile road stated
  twice in the same chapter. Now *four miles down the mountain*.
- **How long the two men have been on the wall — ch 111.** *a fortnight* → *six weeks*.

### Repaired at the Volume 3 Batch 0001 planning pass (fix phase)

Raised by `logs/volume-03-batch-0001.review.log` against the **batch cards**, not the prose. The
planning phase re-dated Volume 3 Batch 0001 from day 2–45 to **day 2–39** and moved the crate from day
35 to **day 28**. The reviewer accepted the new date and asked for two things, both now done. This is
the **first canon change touching a closed volume since the Volume 2 close**, and it is recorded here
rather than made silently, in the same convention as *Date clash — ch 111 vs ch 112* and *Canon change
15 — day numbering extended*.

- **Canon change 30 — the quarter's return is pinned to day 28, and day 0 is the last day of the
  thaw.** Ch 116 says the hours are returned at the quarter, *four weeks off the last day of the thaw*,
  that the crate goes up *on the day and not the one before*, that the *working count is five weeks*,
  and that *Sun He reads a return in the first week after it*. That is day 0 = last day of the thaw,
  crate day 28, reading in the fifth week of summer. **Ch 118 says *five weeks to the quarter's
  return***, and the batch planning pass reclassified that as the **working count** (crate day plus
  Sun He's reading week) rather than as the crate day. **This is the resolution and it stands.**
- **The clash this created, and the word-level repair — ch 116 and ch 118.** Both chapters also said
  *the first return is the second week of summer*, inherited from the Volume 2 batch 0009 cards
  (`outline/batches/volume-02-batch-0009.md`, the ch 116 and ch 118 cards). **No version of the
  calendar supported it** — not the superseded day-35 plan and not day 28; under either, the return is
  read in the fifth week. Repaired word-level: ch 116 *second* → **fifth**; ch 118 *second* → **fifth**
  twice, and *first week* → **fourth week** (the book must hold it in the fourth week for the registrar
  to read it in the fifth). **Ch 118's *five weeks to the quarter's return* is left alone on purpose** —
  it is the working count, it is the number she has been counting by since ch 118, and it is not a claim
  about the crate day. **Do not "correct" it.** Word count unchanged: ch 116 2,083, ch 118 2,604, the
  batch 28,234, Volume 2 111,226.
- **What is now fixed for Volume 3 and may not be moved again.** Day 0 = the last day of the thaw and
  where the season's books close (ch 115). Ch 118 sits in the days immediately after it. Batch 0001 runs
  **day 2 (ch 119) to day 39 (ch 132)**, five and a half weeks. **Ch 129 is the crate, on day 28.** The
  return is *read* in the fifth week of summer and may not be treated as arriving earlier. **Ch 120 is
  the only card that spans two days** (day 4 to day 5; its pull, *a county hour in three days*, is
  counted from the close and lands on ch 121, day 8). **The county hour is not weekly by rule** and no
  drafter may name a weekday. Full table in `outline/batches/volume-03-batch-0001.md` under *In-world
  clock*.
- **Titles.** Ch 123 is **One True Sentence**, changed at the planning pass from *Her Own Hour*, which
  restated a canon line at ch 109 (*she had put it in the quarter's crate on her own hour*). All
  fourteen card titles verified unique against every chapter heading in Volumes 1 and 2.
- **Left alone, deliberately.** The 74 curly apostrophes normalised to straight at the planning pass —
  correct, not damage; the rest of the repository is straight-quoted and this file was the outlier. The
  day-35 → day-28 move itself, which is a planning change and moves no chapter. The Volume 2 batch 0009
  cards, which are historical and still carry the *second week of summer* figure they gave the two
  chapters; they are a record of what was planned, not canon.

### Audited and confirmed (load-bearing for Volume 3)

- **Legs and filings.** Three legs: sponsors' seal **ch 62**, quota clearance **ch 86**, county
  countersignature **ch 115**. Refused twice (ch 70 for want of clearance, ch 88 for want of standing)
  and granted once. **She files once and only once per want** — ch 65 states it in the negative
  (*She did not file anything*) and it holds: two filings in sixty chapters, and the ch 88 pending
  filing is signed in the same hour she is refused.
- **The irreversible decision is hers at ch 88 and is paid at ch 115, not restated.** Sun He: *I
  cannot restore it to you. You have not asked me to, and I would not have done it if you had.* Her
  answer: *I would not have wanted it done.*
- **Yuan's present tense governs his self-reference.** Present throughout 104–118. Statements about
  completed **third-person** events (the bodies in the ground, the eleven, the announcement in the
  winter) keep the past tense — the ch 57 convention, where he says *I have been inside the
  designation for six years and for five of them there was nothing there.* No other character is
  written in a marked present frame.
- **One bracketed line in the closing batch, ch 114.** Ch 104 has none at all. No tribulation anywhere
  in Volume 2.
- **Ledger.** Ch 80 was the only holding; **not retaken anywhere in 104–118.** Ch 118 puts it into a
  standing term she wrote four chapters before Volume 3 opens: *No Ledger, and there is no version of
  the rest of the winter where there is Ledger, and I do not care what it is worth.* **Any move to
  Ledger in Volume 3 is her breaking her own written term, and the price is ~4½ weeks of a channel
  that stands at ~6.**
- **Non-forgiveness runs.** Stated on the page at ch 106, 114, 116 (*Not yet. Not for a long time*)
  and 118, where the refusal to ask him to stop is given a reason that is hers alone: *I have not
  been inside myself once this year without finding you at the bottom of it, and I am not going to
  spend the last of it on a thing that is only mine.* The year still runs into Volume 4.
- **The volume's own span.** The plan called it four months; the manuscript ran to about five and a
  half, from the sixth of December to the last day of the thaw, closing in the first week of summer.
  **The page reconciles with itself** (ch 115's *eleven weeks* against ch 116's *six weeks ago*;
  ch 114's *six weeks to the return* against ch 118's *five weeks*). Recorded, not repaired.

- **Two stages only, one silent and one announced.** **Condensing 3 → 4 at ch 104, SUPPRESSED at her order, no bracketed line in that chapter at all.** Begun in the body ten days after the burial, trained on the Verge flats in thaw mud, one four-pound stone carried thirty yards all day. **He asks first and asks properly** — third term honoured in the correct order, three days held, both choices and both costs laid out, no recommendation — and says plainly that he **cannot know** whether a hole in the chain breaks the filing. Cost: the stage reaches no county book in nine counties, and the county sheet reads one, two, three, then five; **she stops eating for nine days and he cannot make her eat and cannot say why in any way that helps.** **Condensing 4 → 5 at ch 114, ANNOUNCED at her order.** Three weeks under the filing, working the cold end of the furnace floor at Qinghe with nine men two hundred yards off. **First time she is glad of the noise**, because the noise is the record. Cost: the announcement tells nine counties where she is before the ink is dry on her own signature. **No tribulation. No trial. No second Merit source. No Ledger retake.** Arm still at a third throughout.
- **Ch 114 announcement wording, fixed and never to be repeated:** *Assembly Hall of Qinghe. Ning Yan of the Qinghe furnace crew, servant-born, entered on the furnace schedule of this Hall. Nothing further is let go. Condensing, stage five, at Qinghe is held.* Four-clause order; not ch 53, ch 78 or ch 92. **The third clause carries the ceiling** — the layer reports, and what it reports is that nothing further is let go.
- **The six conditions, fixed on the licence at ch 115 and carried into Volume 3.** 1. At every county hour of the filing, in person, in her own hand; **no surety may stand for her again without relicensing** (paid for at ch 106, in writing, stamped, before the licence existed). 2. Hours are those of the furnace schedule of Qinghe, certified by the Hall's clerk, returned at the quarter. 3. Practice at or below Condensing five. 4. No Foundation petition without relicensing. 5. The defect is entered, permanent, and **the licence restores nothing to the aperture**. 6. **The eight hundred jin standing claim is entered on the licence, travels with it, and is not affected by it.**
- **Ch 115 — the gap, ruled.** The county sheet reads one, two, three, and then five. She states it flat and does not ask for it back. **Sun He rules in the open and writes the distinction onto the sheet so the next man does not have to make it himself: an absence is not a breach** — a filing fails when a term is failed; an empty line in a book is not a broken book. The count stands at one, two, three, five; the ceiling holds at five because the ceiling is entered in her hand. **The fourth stage is gone from paper permanently and cannot be restored.**
- **Ch 115 — Sun He.** He could have refused again and says what actually changed: **the labour is on the sheet, and it is worth exactly a stamp and not a thing she has to be careful about on a road.** He then looks at her for the first time and says *you will be careful on a road*. He states once, in nineteen years of that table, the number of people he has examined about their intentions, and it is a number he could put on one hand. The formality is the horror: **the county examines the paper, and the paper is excellent.**
- **Ch 110 — the sealed slip.** Ordinary county stationery with nothing above the county mark, sealed with a grey wafer, came up in the quarter's crate to the **copying floor**, not to the county office. It carries **a request from outside the bound that the term standing in the second position of the status column be struck wherever it is copied within the tower and within the county sheet of the applicant's advancement**, and it identifies the term **by its position and not by its characters**, with an instruction to copy the shape of it, not read it. **The office attached has a name and Yuan cannot read it — the fourth time he has said so, and this time in front of a witness.** Feng states the order of his own three sentences, copies the **shape** of the characters without reading them, strikes the four walled county copies, his working sheet and the shelf copy, and keeps the shape. **She does not ask him to recognise the hand and he recognises nothing; the hand is an ordinary Lowwater county hand and gives Yuan nothing at all. The slip must NOT wear the unrecognised Bureau hand from ch 36.** The **volumes** on the high shelf are not copies, are not struck, and nobody is going to climb four flights to find out. **Nothing answers what became of the eleven.** *Kiln-born* now in **two heads and no paper** — hers and Yuan's. Feng holds a struck shape, not a word. **Column stays at five. Set-aside/unreadable office is spent here.**
- **Ch 113 — the sponsors.** **Lu Kang certifies the furnace schedule in silence**: reads the whole of it, does not look at the nine households, takes the Hall's stamp off the wall and stamps it himself, says nothing. She says to his face that the Foundation writ died on the county sheet the hour she signed the ceiling; Pei An's answer is that it has not been revoked, Lu Kang's is that it has no road left to anywhere. **She does not thank him.** All three remain silent on the dead writ into Volume 3.
- **Ch 117 — Feng's one line.** At the back of a fair volume off the warm shelf: *stage five, at Qinghe, announced; witnessed a countersignature at the county office at Lowwater; copy of the announcement struck under request from without the bound; entered upward with the quarter, entered here, not entered in the column.* **The column stays at five. No audit. No Lung.** He witnesses at ch 115 for four hours, **reads nothing**, writes one line with nothing in it that could be carried upward, and says afterwards that it was like copying.
- **Relicensing, as the manuscript now holds it (audited; gathered for Volume 3, not decided).** A relicensing is **a second application down the same three legs** — sponsors' seal, quota clearance, county countersignature — and it would carry a **continuous hours record from the same origin date as the first**, because ch 115 reads that requirement aloud when it turns the pending filing into a licence. It restores nothing: not the aperture (condition five), not the defect line, not a surety, not a road. What it buys is a **ceiling in her own hand**, which is the same act at ch 88 that killed the sponsors' Foundation writ — and the writ is dead, not revoked (ch 113). **The claim is on the licence as condition six, and Sun He's ch 115 precedent is that a claim in a county book cannot be unlisted by a man stamping a different document and that he is required to enter it** — so a relicensed licence is a licence the claim is re-entered on. Condition two (hours on the Qinghe furnace schedule only) and condition one (every county hour, in person, in her own hand, no surety) are both what make her safe and both are what a relicensing would strain. **Full material in `state/current.md` under `## Volume 3 — the two open structural questions`.**
- **Ch 118 — the Hunter.** A sheet in Feng's fair hand comes down with the crate: a **second cough in the tower stairwell that is not his**, a man at the first desk in the market twice a week who **buys nothing**, **gives his name to nobody**, and **tells the carriers he is not from the Bureau**. **He is unnamed. Bai Zhu is NOT named in Volume 2.**
- **Other chapter-0009 facts.** The **surety's real function** is named once, at ch 106: he was in the room *so that a day could be a day*; with him dead, a missed county hour is a gap and a gap breaks a filing. The **good coat tries the sixth column** at ch 108, is told the fifth is not it and the sixth is, and leaves; at ch 116 he **puts a coin on a shut desk, picks it up, and goes home**. **The girl of twenty-four stays alive** and is at the wash house through ch 109; her hours are four paid out of six, charged to the eighth estate, and she enters the county's schedule request on the quarter's return sheet **on her own hour** (ch 109). Crew **nine**, quarters **ten**, all four days the road. **The intake line stays at the ninth hour; no audit of the change.** The Hall **still has no roof** and the rain goes through it onto the furnace floor schedule in four places (ch 113, 118). **The name in the wrong order is NOT resolved** — the Hall clerk states at ch 118 that he has no other version and no corrected sheet has ever been brought.
- **Titles 104–118:** The Ash-Flats, Nine Days Unfed, Stands to Me, The Quiet Lane, Stone and Paper, The Safest Thing Here, The Sealed Slip, Struck Wherever Copied, Nine Men, The Roofless Hall, Glad of the Noise, Countersignature, The Conditions, Nailed Above the Second Bunk, A Second Cough. All unique against 1–103.
- **Chapter length:** 28,234 words over 15 chapters, average ~1,882. **The guide (1,800–2,700) was met on the average, not per chapter: ten of the fifteen sit below 1,800 individually** — ch 106 1,712 · 107 1,683 · 108 1,765 · 109 1,743 · 110 1,650 · 111 1,752 · 112 1,640 · 113 1,799 · 114 1,743 · 117 1,703 — and the batch carries its length in ch 115 (2,657), 116 (2,083) and 118 (2,604), which are the grant, the conditions and the close. Shortest 1,640 (ch 112); **nothing is under the 1,300 floor**, and the run is not a monotone decline across the batch. No trade-off recorded. **Word count is post-volume-close-repair.** **Do not read the average as a per-chapter precedent for Volume 3** — write each chapter to its own scene and hold the floor.

## Established facts, Volume 3 Batch 0001 (ch 119–132) — EXAMINATION AND EXPOSURE

Volume 3 opens. Fourteen chapters, **36,809 words**, average ~2,629. **Day 0 is the last day of the
thaw** (canon change 30); the batch runs **day 2 (ch 119) to day 39 (ch 132)**, five and a half weeks.
Chapter days: 119 = 2 · 120 = 4–5 (the only two-day card) · 121 = 8 · 122 = 11 · 123 = 14 · 124 = 17 ·
125 = 20 · 126 = 23 · 127 = 25 · 128 = 27 · 129 = 28 · 130 = 31 · 131 = 35 · 132 = 39.

**Repaired after the batch-0001 review.** No fact above moved, and no chapter day moved. The repair
was to the rendering, not the record: seventeen paragraphs were over the ninety-word guide and two
were over the hard hundred-and-twenty ceiling, every one of them caused by single newlines where blank
lines belonged, and all are now split — **in the ten chapters that took nothing but the split, not one
non-blank line differs from what was written.** Longest rendered paragraph is now **90 words**. Two closers were
de-duplicated (0119 / 0121 both ended on *the board was still up*; **0121 now ends on the mat on the
bank**, which is that chapter's own beat), two narrator prolepsises were cut (0126, 0132 — 0132 now
ends on *one coin and one sheet*), one garbled sentence was repaired (0131), and one recap close was
replaced with a landed beat (0126). `workspace/volume-03-batch-0001/SUMMARY.md` had reported a
paragraph check that was false and now states the correction.

**The forward clock, corrected and inherited.** Ch 132 = **day 39**. **Batch 0002 runs day 42 to about
day 130**, not day 120: stage six is six weeks of double-schedule labour after day 39 and lands at
**ch 135 on day 81**; stage seven is five weeks of escort-road work after stage six and lands at
**ch 148 on day 116**; the two weeks of fever stage seven costs carry the close to about day 130.
Pacing inside the batch is uneven on purpose — ch 133–135 carry six weeks, the other thirteen carry
five — and **Batch 0003 opens at about day 133**. Table in `workspace/volume-03-batch-0002/PROMPT.md`.
Arithmetic correction only; no beat moves.

- **Power: nothing. Condensing 5 for all fourteen chapters, ceiling raised to nine by paper at ch 132.**
  No stage reached, no tribulation, no trial. **Zero bracketed layer lines in the batch** — nothing
  announced, nothing listed, nothing killed. **This is deliberate and load-bearing: ch 135's stage-six
  announcement is the layer's first line since ch 114, twenty-one chapters later, and the Hunter hears
  it from the square.** No Ledger, no price named, nothing foreshadowed toward ch 149. No Merit.
- **No romance, no harem, no Wei Ran, no new final enemy.** **Bai Zhu is not named anywhere in the
  batch.** The stranger at the first desk is unnamed throughout and gives his name to nobody.
- **Channel clock.** Ch 118 ~6 weeks (a little under three-quarters) → **ch 132 about three weeks**.
  Stated **once**, at ch 132, in her own arithmetic: six weeks at the bunk in the spring, three now,
  rationed by her own ch 118 conditions (hours only, twice a day, never idled) and it is still three.
  **Do not re-derive before ch 140's kill.** *Recorded, not repaired:* the volume outline's channel
  table expresses these as a rising Settlement fraction (0.75 → 0.82 → 0.70), and ch 118's published
  line reads *a little under three-quarters* of remaining channel. **The two are in tension and the
  page resolves it by speaking only in weeks from ch 121 onward.** Do not put a bare fraction on the
  page again in Volume 3.

### What the batch established

- **Paid work outside the crew, in Lowwater (ch 119).** The lane behind the well: four tables, one man
  with a board, **Da Shan** (minor, no plot function), six loads to a water channel at **twelve coppers
  the load**, counted at the third hour. **The hirer's sheet is not a county sheet** — no office mark,
  no county stamp, nobody certifies or countersigns it, it does not go up a stair and is not copied
  upward. It is chalk and a column and it is wiped at the end of the day. She signs it in her **left
  hand**; the man does not ask about the right one.
- **The county hour (ch 121).** Kept at the **third hour** of the middle of the day at the county
  office on the square. **The lane is nineteen minutes from the square and twenty-one when the square
  is full.** Every county hour therefore costs **four loads and eight coppers — half a day's pay — for
  as long as the road is open.** No schedule she can see can hold the walk. **The county hour is not
  weekly by rule and no weekday is named anywhere in the batch.**
- **The arm, seen early (ch 121).** Yuan tells her the arm is going **before** it goes. This is
  standing **Voice** reach — he reads her, only her, only near her — and **it costs nothing and no
  price is named**. She says once that it is not a favour and is not going to happen often, and he
  says he does it whenever it is true. From ch 121 she knows every time, early, and cannot do anything
  with it. **This runs for the whole volume.** Do not write it as Ledger and do not write it as a cost.
- **Ch 122 — she answers the Hunter.** He asks **who certifies her hours**, in daylight, in a market,
  in front of a queue. **She had four moves available including saying nothing at all and walking on,
  and used none.** She answered: *the Hall's clerk at Qinghe, under a maintaining member's stamp, up
  the quarter's crate, read by the county in the fifth week of summer.* **That answer cannot be
  un-tellen and is on no paper.** Irreversible.
- **The girl of twenty-four (ch 123).** A carrier asked her, by name-less description, whether the
  licensed one at the stone end is licensed by Qinghe or by the county, and she said she did not know,
