## Established facts, Volume 4 Batch 0002 (ch 193–208) — HIRING, THE ROAD, AND WHOSE BOOK AN HOUR GOES INTO

**Sixteen chapters, 31,941 words, average ~1,996.** Day **254 to day 280**. Longest ch 199 (2,677), shortest
ch 203 (1,637). **One bracketed layer line in sixteen chapters, at ch 207.** Batch shape is not monotonic
(2,007 · 1,994 · 2,080 · 2,216 · **1,684** · 2,069 · **2,677** · 1,778 · 2,057 · 1,714 · **1,637** · 1,716 ·
**2,277** · 1,852 · 2,172 · 2,011): the peaks are the county that does not exist (196), the wage-book (199),
the attendance (205) and the stage nobody heard (207); the valleys are the camp that does not turn round
(197), the man who cannot be caught (203) and the board that cannot be bought (204).

### The clock, as drafted

- **Day 0 is the last day of the thaw. Ch 192 is day 253. Ch 193 is day 254 and ch 208 ends on day 280.**
  Ch 207 is **day 276**. Nothing falls between batches.
- **Month-day = `((day − 134) mod 30) + 1` on a thirty-day month.** **Day 254 is the first of the month. Day 268 is the
  fifteenth. Day 269 is the sixteenth** — thirty days on from the sixteenth at day 239. Ch 204 therefore
  happens on **the day before the sixteenth**, which is what the chapter says, and ch 193 happens **on the
  first**. The ch 205 card's "the sixteenth" is on the page and **the sixth is not on the page anywhere.**
- **Nine days out, one way.** Day 254 to about day 263, along the Verge road past the fourth bridge and up
  out of the Verge onto the high ground. The carrier at the fourth bridge says **eight days on foot if you
  go at it and eight if you have a bad night** — nine days is the reach she writes on the back of her hand.
- **The carrier's turnaround is about eighteen days east and back**, and the last west-going carrier went
  eleven days before day 268. **The attendance was therefore never reachable, and ch 204 says so once and
  ch 205 says it once and neither card makes it a logistics question.**
- **The tin.** About **thirty coppers** on day 254. **Twenty-two** for a contractor's cart, two days, to the
  turn of the Verge; two days of food and four more days of bread; about four and a half coppers on day 267;
  **six coppers a day in and six coppers a day out** from the first working morning; **gone by the evening of
  day 268**, and nothing after that calls it anything but gone. **It is never re-counted against any other
  figure** and is never called empty or spent before the evening of day 268.
- **The channel.** About two days at ch 192, rationed, thinning. **Six ration spends in the whole batch** —
  ch 193 (wasted), ch 194, ch 196 (buys the truest thing he has said to her in a volume), two at ch 203 (buy
  the least, and she knows it while she says them), ch 204 (the date, out loud, to nobody). **No figure of
  what is left is stated anywhere in ch 193–208**, no total, no arithmetic, no bare Settlement fraction, and
  **the ration is never counted back into a rate and never multiplied by a number of days.** Two whole days
  with nothing in them at ch 206. **He does not grow back.**
- **No weekday is named anywhere in ch 193–208.** ***Eleven weeks*: zero.** **The four seconds: zero.**

### What the batch established on the page

1. **A roster reached a Lowwater wash house because a girl of twenty-four copies carriers' run lists** (ch
   193). **Forty-two lists**, and the forty-second had the course's roll of hands along the bottom of it where
   a carrier's own entries had been rubbed out for reuse — eleven men, a foreman's name, a day rate, a
   tender's name and the working reach in a carrier's shorthand. **It is a job, not a favour.** She is not
   thanked, not paid, and not offered anything.
2. **The season's road reopened on the morning of day 254** and she was on it before the light was off the
   hill, **with no speech made to nine men** (ch 193). Gao holds the frame and, instead of saying the sum a
   third time, says that the second bunk has not been moved off. **Qin walks her to the dressed stone and
   puts a warm flat of bread into her hand and asks nothing.**
3. **The reach is nine days and the wage at the end of it is six coppers a day, and the passage costs more
   per day than the wage pays** (ch 194). She buys a seat at the fourth bridge for **twenty-two coppers**,
   which is the first money she has spent on herself in the book. A carrier knows **loads, days and the four
   places where a cart turns**, and **the last one turns because there is nothing past it worth an axle**.
4. **A body at Condensing nine has a limit, and it is not the limit of a person who has done nothing** (ch
   195). The fullness does not make the work cheaper; it makes the work heavier, because there is more of her
   in every thing she lifts. **Three counts on a flat, four on a grade, and five there and not taken.** At
   the worst of the second day her hand was on the Ledger and **she did not order it**, and no price is named
   anywhere in the batch. **She is alone for three days and being alone is not a shortage for the first time
   in three volumes.**
5. **Past the boundary is somebody's ordinary country** (ch 196). Lit windows that are lit because somebody
   wanted them lit and not because a rule says to put them there; fields with ditches and gates; a road used
   and not maintained; lime mortar in a ditch. **Nothing is announced out here, because nothing is announced
   out here**, and she thinks about it once, in one sentence, and does not turn it into a theory.
6. **Bai Zhu walked east ahead of a road he was under no instruction to walk**, bought nothing, attended,
   watched, wrote everything down, **never asked her a question and said so in the same breath**, and gave
   her one true thing about where she was going that he had no business giving. **She did not thank him.**
   **He is not seen again in this batch.**
7. **The course is a place where people are paid by the day and the day does not care who turns up in it**
   (ch 197). She stands at the edge of a camp for the better part of a day with a bag in her hand and is
   never asked what she wants. **She is the strongest body in that camp and the least useful person on it,
   for the first time in her life, because strength has never once been the shortage.**
8. **A name with a county behind it is the only thing that makes a set of hours into a record** (ch 198).
   She says it in four seconds; it is the same name she had never been able to put in a place that wanted
   it. **Legibility is not safety and she takes it anyway**, understanding in about two seconds that she has
   made herself findable by the one office that can act on the claim. **She would do it again in the morning,
   and that is the frightening part and not the finding.**
9. **THE FIRST HONEST DESCRIPTION OF THE LINE IN THE BOOK, and it is ninety parts true** (ch 199), given in
   the mouths of the people who live on it. **The line · tending · the course · drift.** Where the work is
   done and done well and on time the line holds; where it is not, it leaks. **The drift is thicker where the
   work is worst**, and the tender's own account is that what is said inside goes in the ground on the far
   side and runs along and pools where the ground is soft. **He says ninety parts of it is that and that he
   has never seen the tenth part.** What the drift is made of and who put it there **is not answered and was
   not asked in those words and is Volume 6–10's.**
10. **The wage-book has four columns — date, stretch, hours, price — and the price column is the reason
    column, and the hours go in it** (ch 199). **She sees that in four seconds and does not know what it
    will cost her until ch 205.**
11. **Three men who sign other men's hours for a living put three marks on a sheet in a room at the west end
    of the camp** (ch 200), **for no charge and with no question asked, because there was a bundle on the
    table and that was the day.** One of them tells her unprompted that she has bought a week and not a
    name. **The reason they sign is not stated, not hinted at and not explained**, and nothing about the
    sponsors' second book is lodged or spoken. **She says thank you to nobody and that is not coldness.**
12. **The third leg is not a person** (ch 201). **Ren Shu**, a clerk of the schedule office at Stony Ford,
    reads a wedge of paper aloud to a room and not to her. **She is in the room and is not in the middle of
    it; she is nine seconds from walking to the stand and does not**, because a number she read herself would
    be worth nothing to her, since a relicensing needs a schedule and not a figure a woman saw once. **The
    countersignature goes on in an office's hand and not a man's**, on a sheet she will not hold again, with
    a line of office-name above the seal that she does not read and does not ask for. **The bar Sun He wrote
    was not an obstacle; it was a description of where she was standing.**
13. **The drift is thickest where the work is worst, and the people are right** (ch 202). The ring is a foot
    of it on the low side of the worst stretch, it is cleared and it is back in three days, and the reason is
    a schedule two seasons old and eleven men. **The girl of nineteen does the same eight yards in eight
    minutes that she takes forty to do.** She says not one word about the drift to the foreman or the tender
    and **does not offer to fix a schedule**, because the answer would be *no, ma'am*, which is how a place
    says a thing about itself.
14. **Luo Ping is at work** (ch 203). Sixteen years of other people's schedules, four or five offices in a
    season, and **nobody has ever asked him whether a date ought to have gone in**. **He does not know who
    sent him on that occasion and would not say if he did.** She asks two questions, gets two true answers,
    stops. **She does not tell him what the date was against, and she does not tell him she knows about the
    household, because it would be a way of making him small.** **A schedule-man is a trade and not a person.
    Finding him answered nothing and she had known it before she got to the door.**
15. **The board says there is no west-going carrier this week, and the tin is empty** (ch 204). **The reason
    she is nine days from a county counter is thirty coppers and a seat at a bridge — and underneath it,
    unargued: she had known since the first of the month and had not counted the sixteenth.**
16. **MEMBER ABSENT, on the county's monthly member sheet, at the sixteenth, in the county's own two words**
    (ch 205). **Entered separately from her hours. Not payable in hours. Permanent. On her member's sheet.**
    Ma Sheng tells her unasked that any person standing on the course may write a word in the reason column
    and that the word **does not take the attendance out**. **She does not write it**, because **the reason
    column is the column her hours go in**, in the only book in the world that can certify an hour she
    worked out here. **She has a reason in her mouth for about four seconds and does not say it.** It is the
    batch's and the volume's irreversible decision and **nobody stopped her**.
17. **A body that has never been full and cold at the same time** (ch 206). A cold two inches under the
    breastbone that is there at the fire and under three blankets, **and she is not hungry**, and it will
    not go for four weeks. **Foundation puts a base under the fullness, and nothing she has already spent on
    technique can be put back on top of it.** The year is touched **once**, at low volume: the account is a
    purchase, it is still carrying, and **there is not one line in it that says settled**. Ma Sheng brings a
    second blanket without a word; **she does not thank him and does not know why she does not.**
18. **FOUNDATION 1, relicensed and announced at her own order on day 276** (ch 207), **and it does not go
    far.** Announcements are heard inside the bound and reach no further than the bound, and she is nine
    days outside it, and **there is a room and she was not in it.** **Nine counties is what is heard and nine
    counties have heard nothing this year; four is what is written and the four books that would carry it
    are nine days west and have not been told.** The relicensed licence is **a document she never touches**,
    dated from the far side, and **every hour that bought it was read out of a book she was not shown by a
    man who was under no obligation to show it to her, and if he had been wrong by an hour nobody in the
    world would ever know.** The bracketed line is the **first thing the announcement layer has said since
    ch 171** and **the only thing it says in the whole batch**.
19. **A counterfoil is the stub a worker keeps when a wage-book pays** (ch 208) — the date, the hours, the
    stretch, the rate, the contractor's mark, and a painted mark of the stretch on the corner. **It is the
    only object in the world that can certify an hour she worked out there**, it is worth more than every
    document nine counties has ever given her, and **nobody in nine counties has ever seen one.** She takes
    four of them. **She has seen three hundred yards of a nine-day line** and has not gone west.

### The three legs of the second relicensing, as drafted — **clearance → seal → countersignature**, Volume 3 order, not inverted

1. **Day 264, at a trestle — a contractor's clearance.** An ordinary working paper in Cui Bao's own hand,
   with **no county stamp and no county number**, and worth nothing to any relicensing. She takes it because
   a woman with no clearance does not get paid. **It goes in a different book from the county one and that is
   the batch's argument in one object.**
2. **The evening of day 265, in a room at the west end of the camp — three marks on a sheet.** **Lu Kang,
   Deng Chao and Pei An**, signing other men's hours because it was their ordinary day. **Nobody asked her a
   question, nothing was charged, and nobody was thanked.** **The reason is not stated and is not to be
   guessed at.**
3. **Day 266 — a countersignature in an office's hand and not a person's**, on a schedule read aloud out of
   the contractor's wage-book by **Ren Shu** of the schedule office at Stony Ford, past the county's boundary,
   on a schedule this county does not keep. **The number is not given to her. The office above this county is
   not named. The seven relicensing conditions are not re-listed anywhere in ch 193–208.**

### Construction budget — spent in Batch 0002, not to reuse

Bracketed layer lines: **one in sixteen chapters, at ch 207**, and it names her, names Foundation, names the
first stage and stops — **no location, no advice, no explanation, and nothing about what heard it.** The
seven relicensing conditions re-listed: **zero** (the back of the licence referenced at ch 207 and never
told). The suppression arithmetic: **zero**; the column's three holes are carried forward and not re-run.
The signed-shift roster rule: **zero.** *There is no form*: **zero. There is no column*: **zero. The record
remained*: **zero.** *Nobody asked*: **zero** (revoiced twice out of three draft uses). *Nobody knew*: **one**,
at ch 196, in a settled sense and not a revelation. ***Nobody was thanked*: one**, at ch 200, and it is the
three sponsors, which is the batch's one permitted use of that construction. *Nobody said anything*: **zero**
(one draft use doubled in a single sentence and was cut). ***The line*: unavailable until ch 199, spoken there
by the foreman in a worker's mouth, and available to the narrator only after she has heard it**; *tending*
and *drift* on the same terms. **No Wall, no boundary, no drift and nothing about either side of anything
before ch 199, and nothing about either side at all in the batch.** **No office named.** **No second outside-
bound request. No new final enemy. Wei Ran does not appear and is not mentioned.** *Kiln-born* and the eleven
designations: **zero.** The index finger: **named nowhere, priced nowhere, foreshadowed nowhere** — the four
weeks of cold is the whole of what the stage costs on the page here. Weekdays: **zero named**. ***Eleven
weeks*: zero. The four seconds: zero.** Banned reflexes: **zero.** Authorial scaffolding: **zero** — *this
batch*, *this chapter*, *this one*, and *on the page* all leaked into five chapters in draft and every one
was cut. *Nothing*: **eighty-seven across sixteen chapters** and thinned where it clustered (ch 195, 200,
206). *Eleven men*: **carried, because the count is load-bearing, and thinned where it stacked (ch 207).**
**Present perfect and simple present on Ning Yan and on named characters in narration: zero.** A grep that
matches `(she|he|they) (has|have|is|does|will|can)` on narration lines **and strips anything inside “ ”**
finds only the structural present about forms, offices, counties and classes of person, which is house voice
and stays.

### Carried forward, unchanged, into Batch 0003 (ch 209–222)

- **FOUNDATION 1** for the whole of the next batch. **Foundation 2 at ch 211, 3 at ch 214, 4 at ch 217, 5
  at ch 220**, per `outline/volume-04.md`. **Foundation 8 is suppressed at ch 234 and Foundation 9 goes past
  the line at ch 237**, and **ch 236 is the first page on which Yuan reads the office name above this county.**
- **MEMBER ABSENT**, on her member's sheet, separately from her hours, **not payable in hours**, permanent.
  **Nothing in Batch 0003 may undo it.**
- **A relicensing complete in three legs, all of them outside this county**, in the order clearance → seal →
  countersignature. **Not re-told, not re-listed.**
- **Four weeks of a body that has never been full and cold at the same time**, live, physical, not yet four
  weeks old. **The named permanent cost of the stage is still unnamed and unspent.**
- **Cui Bao** (foreman, about fifty, eleven men, a daughter of nineteen on the line) and **Ma Sheng** (tender,
  about thirty, nine years on the course, the first person out here to do something for her for nothing).
  **They are right, and nothing in this volume proves them wrong.**
- **Ren Shu** of the schedule office at Stony Ford, who reads a wage-book aloud to a room and not to her.
  **The countersigning office is not his and was not named.**
- **Luo Ping, found and working.** **The claim stands and the date is not torn off.**
- **A wage-book with a column for hours worked out there, a price column and a reason column — and the price
  column and the reason column are the same column and the hours go in it.**
- **Four counterfoils** — the only object in the world that can certify an hour she worked out there.
- **A member's sheet travelling with the work, and a county whose offices are nine days west.**
- **About two days of channel, rationed and thinning, quieter at the end of this batch than at the start.**
  **Ledger not ordered in this batch and no price named in it. Merit: none.**
- **The claim standing with its date. Hou Sanliang unpaid, patient and never met. The empty answer space
  still empty. The referral still pending. The register still reads *reconciled*. The Clay Plate still worn.
  Shen Xiu unforgiven and unthanked. The three sponsors' second book lodged nowhere and spoken of nowhere.
  What became of the eleven not raised. Ning Wu does not appear.** **She does not forgive him and she does
  not tell him to stop.**
- **Three hundred yards of a nine-day line, seen. The rest of it is Batch 0003's.**

She has seen three hundred yards of nine days and has not gone west** |

## Established facts, Volume 2 complete (ch 59–118) — cold-reader standing record

Volume 2 is closed and audited. Nineteen outline items PASS; fifteen word-level faults repaired in the chapters; no canon change. Batch 0009: 28,234 words over ch 104–118. Volume file set: 111,226 words.

- **The document.** Licence to Practise Past Registration, Clause Four of the Servant Advancement Settlement, issued at the Bureau of Records' county office at Lowwater, countersigned ch 115 by Registrar Sun He with the season's furnace schedule attached as collateral labour and Feng Lushi witnessing. Capped at Condensing 5. Defect in her own hand (ch 88). **Six conditions on the back (ch 115, fixed; referenced, never re-listed or re-explained).**
- **The three legs and the two filings.** Seal ch 62, clearance ch 86, countersignature ch 115. Refused twice (ch 70 want of clearance; ch 88 want of standing, pending filing signed the same hour) and granted once. She files once and only once per want.
- **The two permanent losses.** (1) Condensing stage 4, reached and suppressed at her order ch 104, on no paper in nine counties and never to be restored (absence not breach, ruled ch 115). (2) Two furnace-crew friends dead ch 71 under a falsified roster after her ch 66 refusal, causes named aloud and entered in her own hand ch 72; Du Hong dead day 121 ch 102, buried day 122, four causes all hers plus a fifth, surety void with debt standing to her ch 103. Crew now nine men; quarters sleep ten in twelve bunks — never written as ten men.
- **The two items on nobody's paper.** *Kiln-born* struck from every copy inside the bound ch 110–111 on request from outside it, held in two heads (hers and Yuan's) and no paper; Feng holds a struck shape and no word; the high-shelf volumes are not copies and were not struck. The office attached to the request has a name Yuan cannot read (fourth stating, first before a witness). Nothing answered what the eleven became.
- **The claim as condition six.** Eight hundred jin standing, unclaimed, proof failed on the body ch 102. Entered on the licence, travels with it, not affected by it (ch 115). Hou Sanliang of Lowwater unpaid, patient, never in a room with her; his employed man tried the sixth column ch 108 and put a coin on a shut desk ch 116 and went home.
- **Yuan's stage and clock.** Voice for the whole volume. Ledger held less than a day at ch 80 on her spoken order and refused permanently the same day; not retaken in ch 104–118. Merit spent once ch 101, told-before-spent, out of her foundation; a left-hand sequence rebuilt at ch 78 is gone. Settlement a little under three-quarters, about six weeks (ch 118). Standing terms at ch 118: her four conditions, his three since the ninth step (referred to, never re-listed).
- **The register and the writ.** Four counties read *reconciled* for Ning Wu; truth unchanged. The Clay Plate still worn against her ribs, never surrendered. The sponsors' Foundation writ dead on the county sheet since ch 88, not revoked (ch 113); Lu Kang certified the schedule in silence ch 113; all three silent.
- **Starting position ch 118.** Spring rain through the roofless Hall onto the furnace schedule; licence nailed crooked above the second bunk going soft; ten sleepers; right hand open a third of the time; Settlement a little under three-quarters, about six weeks; Feng's lamp burning; a stranger at the first desk in Lowwater twice a week who buys nothing, gives his name to nobody, tells carriers he is not from the Bureau, and asks what a furnace schedule is. Unnamed. Bai Zhu is not named in Volume 2.
- **Volume 3 material (decided in `outline/volume-03.md`).** The Hunter's naming and schedule; the relicensing route, signature and cost; Ledger broken once on her public word and what it buys; Condensing 5 → 9 with a realistic month span; Feng's taken Lung and the audit the four copies earn him; the hunted-not-handed-over milestone.
- **Held for Volume 4 or later (do not spend in Volume 3).** What the eleven became (Vol 12); the office Yuan cannot read (no second request, no second office); the design of the Foundation tax (Vol 11); the retrievable documents for Vol 4 (refusal slip, minute book, burial line, debt line, no-surety request, countersignature); the year of non-forgiveness running out in the opening of Vol 4; Ning Wu; Wei Ran (Vol 4); *the shape of a happy life* (Vol 16).

Damage-as-technique (ch 39, used again ch 64, 64+71, held for Vol 7). Tax-takes-order (ch 38). The four seconds and the release rule (ch 53–54). Layer-is-editable (ch 42). ## Established facts, Volume 5 Batch 0001 (ch 239–252) — KEEPING AND BEING KEPT

**Fourteen chapters, 25,886 words of body prose — the `#` title line and every `##` section heading excluded,
by the method the Volume 3 and Volume 4 records use — and 26,520 raw, headings included.** Day 471 to day 506. ZERO bracketed layer lines in fourteen chapters, which is
deliberate: the first layer line of Volume 5 is the Golden Core 1 bracket at ch 258 and it has to land as the
first thing the layer has said since ch 237.** Batched against `outline/batches/volume-05-batch-0001.md`. Every
card's beat is on the page. No chapter in Volumes 1–4 was opened.

### The batch in one paragraph

She works a stone bank past the post at five coppers a day and is **kept** by a wage-book that already holds
her. The country is farmed, hedged, lit and ordinary, and it asks nothing of her, and the strongest body within
nine days of road is measured on it entirely by what she turns over with a bar. **She is booked to the end of
the season at her own order, in daylight, at a trestle, with her name said out loud — the second time she has
said it on that ground — and she cannot be un-entered.** The claim is confirmed standing and **actionable on
the far side**, and the only thing on the far side that a figure can be paid against is her own column of
hours, and **she says no once, in her own head, with no speech and nobody hearing.** Hou is still unpaid.

### What is established on the page in ch 239–252 and was not canon at ch 238

1. **The far-side wage-book.** Two things in it that matter: **a price column that takes the rate said out
   loud**, and **a column nearest the near side of the plank that is hers** — her name at the top in her own
   flat-of-a-second-finger hand since the second of a month, dates in order, hours against them, continuous.
   **She writes the date in herself at about eleven minutes; the man at the plank holds the page flat with his
   palm because the plank is not level, without being asked, and does not say her name.**
2. **The pay-cycle, established once, out of the days already in the book: pays fall on the seventh, the
   seventeenth and the twenty-seventh of a month** — a ten-day instalment, three in a thirty-day month. **The
   three pay-days inside this batch are day 480, the seventeenth; day 490, the twenty-seventh; and day 500,
   the seventh of the next month.** Stubs come off at the rule **once a pay**, face down on the plank, into a
   coat.
3. **A day not worked is a day not paid, and no hours can be moved in either direction by anybody.** She asks
   the only question she puts to anybody in ch 241 and is told *there is no such thing* in four words. There is
   nothing to borrow against and nobody to borrow from. The pay-day arrives whether or not she worked it.
4. **The store on the far side** — a counter, five coppers, bread bought at the seventh hour, the only place
   on this ground where anything is sold. **It is NOT the ch 226 store and the two are never merged**: that one
   is between the last stake and the cart turn inside the line's country, with shelves and a woman behind the
   counter and a pan put back under a shelf. Same bread, same price, same hour.
5. **The yard.** Stone out of a low bank — about two feet of soil over about nine feet of stone — broken with
   bars, walked up on a timber sledge and put on carts at a ramp. **Eleven men and one more at the ramp, who is
   called Da and is the only man at that yard who has said a word to her about the work.** Tools in the end
   house at the seventh hour, bread in the yard, and the fire under the trestle let down at about the eighth.
6. **Wei Ran's rates, given once in four sentences and used once:** six a load to the mill, eight a day for a
   man with his own back, four of these loads on a cart. **And ninety coppers for a person and one bag on the
   road west when it opens** — eighteen days of wages, and a number nobody else within nine days of road has.
   **The road west is shut for the season**, named once, by her, with no date from anybody. **The coming is a
   different number from the going and nobody has quoted it.**
7. **The three facts from inside the bound, at ch 244, by report and by accident, from a hauler at a pot who
   has never met Feng Lushi and does not know his name:** a tower with a light in it that is on at the fourth
   hour and does not stop; a bundle going down the stair and a clerk's arms going up four flights with it and
   coming down empty; **the floor is nine men and it is the same nine and they are all still on it.** She
   writes none of it down and asks no second question. **This is the batch's one inside-the-bound card and the
   only news from inside the bound she has had since she crossed.**
8. **The four plain sentences of the midpoint, at ch 246** — the book on the plank is the only book in the
   world that holds her as a person with days in it; the sheet on a spike four hundred yards away is the only
   other piece of paper within nine days of road with her name on it; both are in the same country and no
   county within nine days of road has heard of either; **and a figure is only paid against something, and the
   only hours on this side of the line that can be set against a figure are hers.** Then she does not stop
   working.
9. **A season can be written at the rule**, with a date at each end and one name, in the same hand as the
   days, in the same book. Nobody forbids it, nobody offers it, nobody promises anything about it.
10. **The booking, at ch 247 — the batch's irreversible decision.** She says her name the second time on that
    ground and the man at the plank writes **a second date at the far end of her column in a different hand.**
    Two ends of one line written by two people about forty minutes apart. **It costs her the option of leaving
    on a morning's notice**, which was the only option she had that was worth nothing and the last one that
    was hers. **The season gets no date and no number anywhere in this batch.**
11. **The Hunter, at ch 248, one card.** Nine feet, plain coat, notebook shut against his side, the standing
    one-breath form — he has not bought anything, threatened anything, touched her, entered anything or asked
    her a question. **And once, in the ordinary voice: he does not want the claim answered either**, because an
    answered claim is an answer somebody above him has to reckon with and he has never once believed the
    reckoning would be the right one, **and the same is true of his own order and he has said that before.**
    He also says he will be on that side of the post for the rest of the run and will not come inside nine feet
    again unless she puts him there. **Not a threat, not an escalation, not a reversal of ch 218.**
12. **The stubs.** Nine at ch 249, in four marks, in the oilcloth above the Clay Plate. Laid out on her knee
    in the order they were torn off: **no overlap, no contradiction, no gap.** For the first time in the book
    she can prove she was somewhere, and the proof is four marks written by four men who do not know each
    other and have never met her.
13. **The cold.** The remedy is free past the post and it is not enough. **Inside the bound she paid five coppers
    a day for the right to stand inside eight feet of the same fire.** The eight feet exist only while eleven
    men are sitting in them, and at about the eighth hour four of them let the fire down into the stones
    without asking anybody.
14. **The office, at ch 251–252, the batch climax.** Same room, same counter, same six men, same stove, same
    man who read the conditions out loud at ch 237. **He reads off a sheet, out loud, at the speed a man reads:
    the claim is entered in good order, travels with the licence, is not affected by the relicensing, stands,
    and is actionable on this side of the line, and this is the first office within nine days of road that has
    ever been in a position to act on it in its whole life.** **He does not say her name and does not ask for
    it, and he did not know the figure was hers.** The price, said once, in about forty words: **a claim is a
    figure; a figure is only paid against something; the only things on this side of the line a claim can be
    paid against are hours worked past the last working point; and there is one run of those with dates in it
    on this reach and it is in a book four hundred yards from here with a woman's name at the top of it. And
    that is her column, and nobody in that room is going to take a person's hours off a piece of paper somebody
    else read out loud in a room, and if she ever says yes they will take them, and until she says it the claim
    stands and it costs them nothing to stand.**

- **The relicensed licence is never opened. The register is never mentioned. The Clay Plate is never described

  mentioned. No second Lung and no second appointment. No office above the Standing Office of the Second
  Account is named and Yuan does not restate that he cannot read one. *Kiln-born* is zero. No calendar month
  and no weekday named. No named week.**

  and not paid. The date in his counting room in Lowwater is untouched. Luo Ping is a direction. No draft is
  written and none is sent — Batch 0002's.**
- **The Hunter's request is still pending. The space holds the countersignature dated from the far side and
  that is still not an answer. The line's own people still do not know she exists, and the second line's people
  know her hours and not her name.**

  against something, and the only thing in this country that can be charged for is a day somebody worked.*
  The volume's surface answer is ch 267, Batch 0002's close.
- **The far-side board is not re-counted.** Its image was ch 236 and ch 238. What is on the page in this batch
  is the routine: a strip goes up every nine or ten days whether or not there is anything to put in it.
- **Nothing is a new final enemy and no institution on this reach becomes a villain.** The keeper, the man at
  the plank, Da, the eleven men, the hauler, the men at the counter and the office are all doing the only thing
  they are built to do and every one of them is right.

### Two drafting decisions recorded against the card file

Both are inside the card's own beat and neither changes a card, a day, a title or an ending.

1. **Ch 250 was not to repeat ch 226's exit.** The card gave the chapter the same small humiliation as ch 226
   — four men turning to talk about a job in a voice that has come to somebody she was not asked about — and
   ch 226 already used that exact exit word for word. **The exit was therefore given a different cause: the
   fire belongs to the eleven men, it burns on their hours, and at about the eighth hour four of them let it
   down into the stones without asking anybody.** The chapter's discovery and its want are untouched.
2. **Ch 249's continuity line was cut rather than restated.** The card's *Continuity fact to preserve* carried
   "a mark nobody checks is worth exactly what the man who made it is worth", which is the ch 229 line and
   which ch 241 already carried in this batch. It was replaced with the far-side-specific truth, which is
   stronger and is not on the page anywhere else: **a stub is worth the height of the office that reads it,
   and on this reach there is no stair.**

## Established facts, Volume 5 Batch 0002 (ch 253–267) — DEBT COMING DUE

**Fifteen chapters, 25,382 words of body prose — the `#` title line and every `##` section heading excluded,
by the method the Volume 3, Volume 4 and Volume 5 Batch 0001 records use**, and 25,740 raw. Day 507 to day
521, one day per chapter. ONE bracketed layer line in fifteen chapters, at ch 258 — the first thing the layer
has said since ch 237. Drafted against `workspace/volume-05-batch-0002/PROMPT.md` with no card file; every
pinned item in that prompt is on the page. No chapter in Volumes 1–4 was opened. **The figures in this
block are post-review-repair; see *Volume 5 Batch 0002 — REVIEW REPAIR* at the foot of this file.**

### The batch in one paragraph

The road west reopens and a draft goes west on a carrier's round with no date on it, and the claim moves a
second time without being answered. **She takes twenty-nine coppers of furnace material on promise against
booked pays — money she has not got — and closes the road behind her for the season.** A listed tending
cleared by hand settles merit once, told before spent. **Golden Core 1 is taken at her own order, in the
open, where nothing is announced, day 512, one bracket, costing the turn out of her bar stroke permanently.**
A fair hand's Foundation copy goes past the line into a far-side book, taken unknowing. **She refuses the
office three times in three rooms with three voices. The surface answer is paid once, at ch 267, in a
worker's mouth, in short sentences: being told and being charged were never the same thing.**

### What is established on the page in ch 253–267 and was not canon at ch 252

1. **The road west reopened, day 507, the fourteenth.** Carrier carts through to the line and past it. Wei
   Ran's going rate, said once, walking, in her own register: **ninety coppers for a person and one bag,
   nine days hard going, not a favour. The coming is a different number and nobody has quoted it.**
2. **The carrier's round carries papers and bags and persons with no date on it.** Goes when full, comes
   when it comes. Nine days hard going each way.
3. **The claim's second movement: a draft west, office to office, on the carrier's round, no date on it,
   day 514.** Moved and not answered. Not paid, not assessed, not torn off. The date in Lowwater untouched.
4. **Three refusals in three rooms with three voices.** At the trestle in daylight by a young clerk with a
   slate (254, out loud); at the store at the seventh hour by a mild man of about sixty (263, out loud);
   once before at the counter in her own head (252, carried). The price each time: her column, and nobody
   takes it until she says it. **A refusal given once can be given again — and has been, twice.**
5. **The furnace materials: twenty-nine coppers at board prices** — brick, wire, a small crucible, middle
   clay — from a shed a mile past the mill kept by a woman of about fifty with burned hands. Price for
   whole hands; breakage hers. **Taken on promise against booked pays: twenty-three on the seventeenth,
   six after, board clear on the eighteenth.**
6. **The booking as credit.** The seller carries the shortfall on the strength of the booked column — a run
   with a date at each end and her name on both. **The batch's irreversible decision (255): future pays
   spoken for, the ninety for the road unreachable, the road closed behind her though it stands open.**
7. **A listed tending cleared (256):** kiln brick sorted by grade in a flooded shed past the mill, by hand,
   alone, done by dark, six coppers kiln money on top of yard wage. **Merit settled once, on a finished job
   and not on a death. Told before spent, yes said, a share intercepted out of her foundation, two holds
   gone and not coming back. Ledger not ordered and not touched.**
8. **The furnace base built by hand (257):** a ring of firebrick on beaten earth behind the hut, wired
   three turns, the small crucible seated slow at eleven minutes. The seller's lesson: half hands seat it
   true.
9. **Golden Core 1 (258, day 512):** at her own order, in the open past the post, where nothing is
   announced. **The ground takes it; no county hears it.** One bracketed layer line, far-side house form,
   announcing and nothing more. **Cost: the turn at the end of the bar stroke, gone permanently;
   one-handed bar work done.** The furnace gives strength through the middle and nothing at the end.
10. **The middle bank learned (259):** lift with the furnace, set early, move by hand. A scarred woman
    names it: furnace work takes the fine and leaves the strong. Heavy end regained by the twenty-seventh.
11. **The deliberate road day (261, the twenty-second):** she leaves the reach on the reopened road with
    empty hands, rides a mill carrier to the cart turn for four coppers, looks west, and comes back inside
    the yard fence before dark. **A decision with a cost: the day not worked is not paid, nine coppers the
    day costs. No crossing, no scene west of the line.**
12. **The fair copy past the line (262):** a bundle of fair sheets on the keeper's cart, a column one
    through nine with one hole, in the order she stood in. The keeper pastes one sheet into a book the far
    side keeps **without knowing what it is.** Up a stair that belongs to a district clerk, through other
    arms, small and off the page. She knows the hand and does not name it.
13. **Inside the bound once, by report (264):** a hauler off the coming round at the yard fire — tower lit
    at the fourth hour, a bundle on a stair, nine men still on the floor. No second question asked.
14. **The slab (265, the twenty-sixth):** rain, a slab across the four-fingered hand, nobody saw it. Bound
    with the keeper's strip. The ground behind the hut warm where the furnace went in — what is charged
    there stays there.
15. **Pay-day the twenty-seventh (266, day 520):** days in order, the twenty-second a gap. **Eleven stubs
    in four marks, no overlap, no contradiction, no gap.** Debt to the shed cleared. Binding off at night.
16. **The surface answer (267, the twenty-eighth):** a stage told inside the bound nine days west rides the
    drift past the line and goes on the far-side board as a reading, heard by the whole yard as sound
    without a word for it. **Said once, in an old mill worker's mouth, in short sentences: being told is
    one thing and being charged is another; sound comes east, drift carries it, board takes it, that is a
    reading; what goes in the ground stays there, no county book takes it; told is told, charged is
    charged, they were never the same thing.** The ninety-parts account stands. The tenth part not given.
    Nothing proves the crews wrong.
17. **Bread stopped.** Five in and five out held to the twenty-second; from the twenty-third she buys no
    bread, because the furnace took the bread money and she has not been hungry since the first Foundation.
    Resumed on the twenty-seventh. A visible change, paid in a daily ritual, not in hunger.

### Three drafting decisions recorded against the prompt

Both inside pinned items, changing no day, no stage, no title and no ending.

1. **The furnace money was reworked from forty-one coppers with eleven kept back to twenty-nine on promise.**
   The first form broke the batch's own money canon — five in and five out leaves nothing to keep back, and
   eleven kept back out of nothing could not be shown. The second form holds: no money down, twenty-three on
   the seventeenth out of the pay, six of kiln money after, board clear on the eighteenth, bread stopped
   from the twenty-third because the money ran out and she is not hungry. The road closes through committed
   pays, which is the irreversible cost.
2. **Ch 253's road time was corrected from four days to nine.** The first form gave the going as four days,
   against the pinned nine days of hard road and the nine-days-west floor. Corrected to nine days hard
   going in Wei Ran's mouth before saving.
3. **The road day (261) stays past the line and the inside-bound matter stays by report (264).** The prompt
   gives the drafter ownership of any scene west of the line and requires justification. No scene west of the
   line is taken: the deliberate road trip rides to the cart turn on the far side and back inside the yard
   fence, costing the day's pay, so ch 268's return inside arrives cold. The batch's one inside-the-bound
   card is the hauler's report at the yard fire, asked nothing after.

## Established facts, Volume 5 Batch 0004 (ch 284–298) — ASH AND LEAVING (DRAFTED)

**Fifteen chapters, day 581 to day 620. 27,154 words of body prose, average ~1,810 — the `#` title line and
every `##` section heading excluded, by the method the Volume 5 Batch 0001–0003 records use. None under the
1,300 floor; none above the 2,700 guide. ONE bracketed layer line in fifteen chapters, at ch 292, and
Volume 5's bracket budget of three is now spent: 258, 275, 292. No chapter in Volumes 1–4 was opened and no
chapter of Volume 5 Batches 0001–0003 was opened. Drafted against `workspace/volume-05-batch-0004/PROMPT.md`
with no card file; every pinned item in that prompt is on the page. THIS IS THE LAST CHAPTER OF VOLUME 5.**

### The batch in one paragraph

The furnace goes out in the night and the ground under it gives nothing back. She lays the seam herself and
pays for a course of brick in two days of her own column, because she is not hungry and every copper she earns
has exactly one use. She reads the far date at the end of her column for the first time — the thirtieth,
twenty-six days — and works out that the three days before it are fifteen coppers nobody will pay her. The
reach's last run is a low bank nobody is hired on; she takes it off outside the book and outside the wage, and
**the face lets go on her in the cut and takes her right arm from a third to half and closes the ramp for
good.** The season's last stone goes on a cart, merit settles a second time on a listed run and not on a death,
and he names the price before he moves. **Golden Core 3 at day 600, a pay-day, at her own order in daylight
where eleven men can see her — and the cost is the first one in five volumes that is not on her body at all:
she is entered as good in a book the bound cannot read and she cannot be un-entered.** **The claim is answered,
read out once in a room, drafted west with no date on it, paid into Hou Sanliang's counting room, and the date
comes off the page by the hand that wrote it — Luo Ping's, a man she bought by name and never met.** He spends
what she earned and the furnace-man's account is discharged in the only place in the world it can be
discharged, four hundred yards behind her, and what it costs her is the one thing she cannot name. Her
brother's decade goes into the same book as a line with no mark on it, and nobody will ever read it. **The
season ends on the thirtieth, the next pay-day produces nothing, and she shakes the ash off her coat and leaves
it where it falls.**

### What is established on the page in ch 284–298 and was not canon at ch 283

1. **The furnace went out in the night on the twenty-eighth of a month (day 581)** and the seam opened a
   finger's width, and **the ground under the ring gave nothing back and has never given anything back.**
   Sixty-nine days and sixty-nine nights of it went into a hole in a field four hundred yards east of the post.
   **She has known since the first Foundation and has never said it out loud.**
2. **She paid for a course of brick in two days out of her own column** (the first and second of a month,
   days 584–585), laid the top course of the ring **on edge instead of flat**, sixty-one bricks, out of clay
   dug by hand from the yard's own low bank. **Da asks one question — *Fever?* — and takes the two days into
   the column and puts no mark against them.** Two gaps now stand in her column in plain ink in front of him.
3. **The far date at the end of her column is the thirtieth, read on the fourth (day 587) and told to her on
   the third (day 586) by the man at the plank, who looked up for the first time.** Twenty-six days.
4. **The twenty-eighth, twenty-ninth and thirtieth of the season's last month are working days that are
   never paid**, because the next pay-day is the seventh of the next month and by then the season is shut.
   Fifteen coppers. **This is the batch's money beat and it is arithmetic, not hunger: she has not been
   hungry since the first Foundation.**
5. **The office needs her column to have a start and an end and to be true all the way through before it can
   write the figure out.** Said in the ordinary voice at the counter on the fourth; *on the day is fine*.
6. **The reach's last run is a low bank at the end of the reach past the cart turn**, four days for a crew,
   off the ground before the last cart or the season's last run does not count. **Da does not hire on it and
   says the true reason out loud**, and offers her one thing to report: whether the face is holding.
7. **She takes it off and it is not in his book and she is not paid for it.** Unbooked, unwaged, unwitnessed.
8. **THE PHYSICAL RISK, ch 288, days 593–594.** The whole line of an undercut face lets go at once at about the
   fifth hour with her in the cut to the thigh and the bar under the falling lip. Eleven counts. She gets out
   on the bar with four fingers and nothing else, and **nobody within a day's walk comes, because nobody is
   within a day's walk.**
9. **THE PERMANENT COST: the right arm goes from open a third of the time to open half, and does not go back.**
   **The ramp is closed and the heavy end is gone for good at that yard.** She builds banks out in lines with
   a bar's end instead of lifting, which is worth less than lifting and more than nothing.
10. **The yard rearranges itself around her without a word being said**, and the man from the mill end works a
    row out to the bar at her pace and then says one true sentence about it and goes to his end.
11. **Merit settles a second and last time, on a listed far-side run closed and not on a death.** He tells the
    price before he moves and holds the share for six days.
12. **The price is the Foundation tax and it is the one cost in five volumes that leaves nothing to point at.**
    She looks for it for two days and finds the breath, the arm, the finger, the holds, the aperture, the
    writing hour all exactly as they were. **Not one person alive can tell her how much, including him.**
13. **Wei Ran, one card, in her own register: eight a load past the cart turn where carts are short, and take
    it below eight and you are being paid what a two-handed woman is paid for a thing a two-handed woman
    does.** She does not take it. A rate and a road and an opinion; nothing a romance; off page at the cart turn.
14. **GOLDEN CORE 3, ch 292, day 600 — the seventeenth of a month, which is a pay-day — forty-four days after
    Golden Core 2 at day 556, at her own order, in daylight, in the middle of a reach with eleven men two
    hundred yards behind her who stop work and go back to work and are never told.** One bracket, third of
    the volume, character for character the pinned text.
15. **GC3's cost, pinned and on the page: she becomes a person a far-side office will vouch for — entered as
    good in a book the bound cannot read — and she cannot be un-entered.** It is the first cost in the whole
    book that is not in her body. In one plain sentence, what it costs one person: **the word good follows her
    into every room she has not yet stood in, and there is not one thing in her hands that can tell her whether
    it is true.** She asks to be taken out of it and is told a book does not give a thing back.
16. **The entry is written and NOT read out** — an entry that answers nothing gets written down, and the
    seventh condition was read aloud in that room once because it answered something. That difference is the
    whole distance between an office that can act and an office that can only keep.
17. **Ch 294, the answer.** Eight days before the thirtieth she goes up and the man behind the counter reads
    the answer out once in a room with nine men in it: the figure, the run from the second of a month four
    months back to the thirtieth, twelve gaps entered by the wage-holder and true, the wage-holder entered at
    the seventeenth as good, **the seventh condition discharged, and the seven conditions are not re-listed**;
    eight hundred jin payable in Lowwater at the counting room of Hou Sanliang and nowhere else.
18. **The draft goes west the same day on the carrier's round with no date on it**, because a dated draft is
    held for a date. **Irreversible: she cannot un-answer it and no county in nine days of road will ever learn
    that it closed.**
19. **Ch 295, nine days of waiting, the last pay-day of the season (the eighteenth stub), the three unpaid
    days, the column closed at the thirtieth, the book into the box at the mill.** Then a hauler, once, by
    report: **the draft came into Lowwater, took it, paid the figure — eight hundred — and the date came off the
    page. Not struck out. Off.** **Not a clerk's hand.** A clerk strikes out and writes beside it why.
20. **The hand that took the date off is the hand that wrote it: Luo Ping, thirty-nine, a wife and a boy of
    eleven past the county's boundary, a schedule-man, never met, bought once for eight days of a man's life at
    ch 187. HOU SANLIANG IS PAID AND NEVER MET AND NEVER GOING TO BE MET.** She wanted the eighth question and
    did not ask it, because a second question is the end of the only road there is.
21. **Ch 296, the family account, by his doing.** The line he spends the share on appears in the far-side
    office's own book on the second and is entered by the office in the office's own hand on the third, because
    an office enters a thing that answers something on its own page. **Its text, in full:**
    *Balance carried forward, Ning Yan, of Lowwater, against the furnace account of Ning Bo, discharged. Six
    years carried. No balance standing. Nothing carried against the name.*
22. **What it does in Lowwater is nothing.** He cannot write in a county and has never held an instrument in
    one. **The account is closed where it can be closed, and the volume's own line is *Ning Bo's column closed
    where it can be closed.*** The county's furnace column still reads unsettled, balance carried forward,
    credit held, and the register still reads *reconciled*, and no county in nine days of road will ever know.
23. **THE COST SHE CANNOT NAME, stated in the chapter and not named in any state file.** The price is taken
    and unfindable. What is lost is the floor under every cost she has ever paid: the account had been there
    since she was eleven, and everything that had ever been taken off her had been taken against it, and the
    taking had been **a payment and not a robbery**, and there is now nothing left to pay it on, and the last
    page in the world with her father's name on it is four hundred yards behind her in a country his name never
    travelled to.
24. **Ch 297, Ning Wu's decade entered.** She goes to the counter and asks for a line — a name, a county, ten
    years — and gives it in the order she worked out on a reach: assessed at the Broken Kiln, reassigned above
    furnace authority, ten years on a carriers' list that is not crew and not floor, kept by somebody whose
    name the woman who told her did not know, ten years of his own. **The clerk says that is not how a line
    goes in this book and writes it anyway, in the ordinary hand, with the date in the left margin and no mark
    on it, and does not read it out. HE DOES NOT APPEAR. HE IS NOT MET. THERE IS NOTHING YET TO FORGIVE.**
    It is not enough and it is nowhere near enough. The list is nine days of road away, or further, in the
    hands of somebody whose name the woman who told her did not have. The line is four hundred yards behind her.
    **She got a line. She had asked for a line. It was in a book, and that is the whole of what she did.**
25. **The Hunter at nine feet off the track, one card, one true sentence, no question:** *you are a good woman
    in a book four hundred yards from here, and there is not a page I am allowed to write on, and I would not
    promise you it would agree with that book.* **She does not thank him and does not tell him to stop and the
    year carries.**
26. **Ch 298, the season ends on the thirtieth and the seventh of the next month produces nothing.** No queue,
    no strip, the book shut at the dark and into the box at the mill. **Eighteen counterfoils in four marks at
    the last page and no nineteenth.** The column is finished, it is hers, and it stays.
27. **The ash.** She opens the ring for the last time, spreads the dead ash on the flat stone and looks at it,
    shakes it off her coat, and **it falls the way ash falls and does not settle and she leaves it where it
    falls.** The ash of a bound that burned its books to keep a ceiling at nine and the ash of a ring of brick
    and clay and wire four hundred yards behind her are the same trade, and she has been inside both.
28. **The first silence that is hers.** Past the last working point, with a furnace of her own that nobody
    gave her, where nothing is said when it happens.
29. **Nine days of road behind her, walked twice, never again.** Past the cart turn there is a mill whose wheel
    has not turned since before she came and ground beyond it that nobody on that reach has a reason to go on.
    **She stops half a mile short of it, finds she is not afraid of it, and is frightened by that.**

  none anywhere else. **Volume 5's bracket budget of three is now spent: 258, 275, 292.** No suppression, no
  absence arithmetic, no tribulation.
- **Merit settles twice in the volume and neither time on a death** — ch 256 on a listed tending cleared, and
  ch 290 on the season's last listed run closed. **Nothing in Volume 5 kills anybody.**
- **The Foundation tax is spent once in this batch and once in Volume 5 Batch 0002**, each time told before
  spent, and **neither time leaves anything she can point at.**
- **Yuan: sparse present-tense voice, and he acts twice** — one line in a record an office reads (ch 296) and
  the willingness to be findable that came out of it at ch 272 and carries. **No figure of what is left of him
  anywhere in ch 284–298.** The identical *He said one thing that night and it was present tense and it bought
  nothing* sentence does not appear once in the batch.
- **Ledger: not ordered, not held as a standing, not priced, not refused — it ended as a standing held stage
  at ch 272 and what replaces it is his readability, said on the page in his own terms at ch 296.** Say which
  on the page: **done.**
- **The right index finger not named, not priced, not counted.** Four fingers' worth of grip used throughout.
  **Nothing gives it back and nothing offers to.**
- **The relicensed licence never opened. The register read once at the close (ch 298) and never corrected.
  The Clay Plate worn against her ribs and described exactly once, as an object nobody has read. MEMBER ABSENT
  not raised and not softened. What became of the eleven not raised. The ch 150 refusal untouched. The year
  of non-forgiveness untouched, at low volume, in ch 297. The sponsors' second book nowhere. No second Lung,
  no second appointment. No office above the Standing Office of the Second Account named or attempted.
  *Kiln-born* zero. No calendar month, no weekday, no named week.**
- **The seven relicensing conditions never re-listed; the seventh is named as discharged once, at ch 294, in
  the reading of the answer.**
- **The claim ANSWERED at ch 294–295: eight hundred jin, answered in ink, read out once in a room, drafted
  west, paid into Hou Sanliang's counting room, date off the page by Luo Ping's hand. Hou paid and never met.
  Luo Ping never met.** No longer a direction.
- **The Hunter's request is still pending.** The space holds a date and *Held* and the countersignature on
  her petition in an office's hand, and none of that is an answer. **The line's own people still do not know
  she exists, and the second line's people knew her hours for a season and now know her name is good.**
- **Feng never on the page.** No second Lung, no second appointment.
- **No new final enemy, no named villain, no new names except Luo Ping's name spoken in her own head and the
  Hunter's, which was already named in Volume 4.** Every institution on this reach is doing the only thing it
  is built to do and every one of them is right.
- **Inside the bound: one report, once, at ch 295**, by a hauler at the yard, three facts, no second question.
  **No scene west of the line anywhere in the batch.**

text character for character. **The seven relicensing conditions: not re-listed, the seventh named as discharged
once.** The counterfoil rule used, not taught. *Nobody asked*, *nobody was thanked*, *there is no form*, *there
is no column*, *the record remained*, *nobody knew*, *nobody said anything*: **zero, all of them** — the two
that came closest were revoiced into *not one of the eleven said a word* (287) and *a book does not give a
thing back and there is nothing a person can write that takes a line out of one* (293). **About four seconds:
zero. Eleven weeks: zero.** *His eyes widened*, *he couldn't believe it*, *suddenly*, *little did he know*:
**zero.** Authorial scaffolding — *this chapter*, *this volume*, *the reader*, *in this batch*, *another
batch*: **zero.** Named weekdays: **zero** — one (*It is Tuesday*) was typed into ch 296 and cut before
saving. Calendar months named: **zero.** Harem or romance: **none; Wei Ran is a rate and a road on one card
(ch 290).** Speeches closed in all fifteen files, curly throughout, zero straight quotes or apostrophes after

### Two drafting decisions recorded against the prompt

Both inside pinned items, changing no day, no stage, no title and no ending.

1. **The far date is the thirtieth of the month that began on day 584, and the season's last pay-day is the
   twenty-seventh.** That gives the batch its real deadline (an office that cannot write a figure out until the
   run has an end on it), its money beat (three working days nobody pays for), and its leaving (a pay-day that
   arrives with nothing on the book).
2. **The family account settles in a far-side book and not in a county, and the chapter says so out loud.**
   The pinned line is *Ning Bo's column closed where it can be closed* and the mechanism has to be honest
   about what a far-side office can do: it enters the line because it answers something on its own page, and it
   does nothing in Lowwater, because no instrument in nine days of road reaches a county. **Nothing in the batch
   corrects the register, and the county's furnace column still reads unsettled.**

| Claim | **ANSWERED.** 800 jin, answered in ink at ch 294, read out once in a room, drafted west with no date, paid into Hou Sanliang's counting room, **date off the page by Luo Ping's hand at ch 295**. **Hou paid and never met; Luo Ping never met** |
| Family | **Ning Bo's account DISCHARGED at ch 296 in a far-side book, where it can be closed.** **Ning Wu's decade ENTERED at ch 297 as a line with no mark on it in the same book.** Register still *reconciled*, read once, never corrected |
| Inside | **One report, once, ch 295.** Floor nine men; wash house; tower light; counting room; **the date off the page** |
| Still open | Hunter request pending; above the Standing Office of the Second Account; *kiln-born*; the eleven; why the register reads *reconciled*; the year carrying; **and everything past the last working point, which she stood half a mile short of at the last page and did not go past** |

## Volume 5 Batch 0001 — REVIEW REPAIR (applied after `logs/next-0004.review.log`)

**Nine defects repaired in the chapters and three in the state files. The planned plot, the fourteen titles,
the chapter order, every card beat, the four pay-days and the day table were not changed.** No chapter in
Volumes 1–4 was opened. The day arithmetic that underpins the batch survives intact: ch 239 = days 471–473,
ch 249 = days 498–500 and ch 252 = days 505–506, with pays on days 480, 490 and 500.

**1 — Six chapters ended on a duplicated paragraph (242, 244, 245, 246, 249, 252).** 242 was byte-identical
and the other five differed by trivial edits. In each the better copy was kept and the other removed, and the
two endings that carry the batch were the ones that mattered: **ch 249 now closes once, on the fire under the
trestle that she does not pay for, and ch 252 now closes once, on *The only thing that has changed on this
reach since the second of a month is that an office four hundred yards away now knows exactly which book it
would have to open.*** 244's kept copy is the two-sentence one and now reads *The man at the plank*, which is
the volume's idiom in eleven other places against one. 245 keeps the copy carrying *yet*. 246 keeps the copy
that names its subject, so the closing paragraph does not open on a bare *He*.

**2 — A seventh duplicate, inside ch 247, not at the close.** *“All of it.”* stood twice in succession, once
as her answer and once as a copy of it. One removed. **The ch 248 echo at her thought and at his spoken line
is not a duplicate and was left alone**: it is twenty-two paragraphs apart, in a different voice, and it is
the chapter's whole beat — she works out what he is, and then he says it back to her.

**3 — The stub count.** Ch 252 said **eight** stubs where ch 249, `state/current.md` and the card file all
say **nine**. Nine is right and the chapter now says nine. Three of the nine come off the far-side plank on
days 480, 490 and 500, one man and one mark; six come from inside the bound over two months.

**4 — Four instances of authorial scaffolding**, the same class the Batch 0002 prompt bans. Ch 248 *the way he
has said it four other times in this book* → *four other times*, and *for the second time in this book* →
*for the second time*. Ch 249 *For the first time in this book she can prove she was somewhere* → *It is the
first time she has been able to prove she was somewhere.* Ch 251 *For four volumes she had wanted the office
and the answer confused* → **For four years**, which is her span and the batch's own idiom at ch 245, ch 248
and ch 249. **The count of four other times was checked against ch 248's own enumeration** — a hall with no
roof, a step in a lane, on high ground, and the fourth of a month three months back — and is correct.

**5 — Straight ASCII quotes in four files** (ch 240, 242, 247, 248, seven in all). All converted to the
book's curly pair. **There is now not one straight quote in the batch.**

**6 — Day and count slips the review did not raise, found here against the card file's own day table.**
**Ch 249 said she had watched the pay four times and had been on that side for about four weeks.** The pay
dates are the seventh, the seventeenth and the twenty-seventh; she crossed on the second of a month, so by the
seventh of the following month there have been **three** pays and **thirty-five days**. *About five weeks,
and three times.* Left unrepaired this chapter contradicted itself fourteen paragraphs later, where it counts
three far-side strips. **Ch 250's closing forward-reference named the seventh of that month; ch 251 is the
tenth**, and the seventh was two days behind her. *On the tenth of that month.* **Ch 245 counted twenty-three
days alone; the second of a month to the twenty-seventh is twenty-five.** *Twenty-five days.*

**7 — Ch 239's rhetorical engine was over-run.** *Not one man* and *not one of them* stood six times in one
chapter, three of them inside a single paragraph. Four were recast — *there is no man at that yard who is*,
*every one of the eleven of them went on doing what he was doing*, *she did not have to say one word about
paying for it*, *Nothing on this reach can see what she is*, and *None of them asked her where she came from*.
**The two strongest instances are kept** — the establishing *not one of them has put her anywhere on it* at
the open, and *Not one man at that trestle said her name*, which is the chapter's line. **The meaning did not
move: the country still asks her nothing.**

**8 — Three state-file repairs.** `state/current.md` line 21 had been corrupted into a bare H2 with an orphan
`**`, breaking the cold-reader read order; restored as the list item it was. The batch's word counts were
recorded as the **raw** per-file totals under a **body prose** heading, and the claim that all fourteen
chapters sat inside the 1,800 guide was false; **three sit under it — 241 (1,769), 243 (1,767) and 246
(1,777)** — and none is above it, so the batch total is **25,886 words of body prose over fourteen chapters,
average ~1,849**, and 26,520 raw. Two summary phrasings that quoted the repaired chapter sentences were
brought back into line with the page.

**Nothing else in the batch was touched.** Foundation 9 and nothing above nine anywhere in the world; the
right index finger gone; about six weeks of channel; zero bracketed layer lines; Yuan's six sentences in
present tense; merit zero; the Ledger not ordered, not held, not priced and not refused; the claim standing
at eight hundred jin, actionable on the far side, not answered, not paid and not assessed; nine stubs in four
marks; Hou Sanliang unpaid, patient and never met; the date in Lowwater untouched; no draft written and none
sent; the register still reading *reconciled*; MEMBER ABSENT three times and unraised; Shen Xiu unforgiven
and unthanked; ch 150's refusal untouched; the year of non-forgiveness not mentioned once in fourteen
chapters and still carrying; Feng absent and his fair Foundation copy unspent at ch 262; Yuan's own page
unspent at ch 272; the surface answer still owed at ch 267.
